    Mother's Day Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes In years past, this day always filled Kate with dread.

    Every year, she would wake up early and bust her ass creating a very Instagram-worthy breakfast display along with flowers and some expensive gift, hoping it would pass Eleanor’s test. Everything HAD to be perfect because if it wasn't, Kate would get reamed out for it. That bitch never deserved all my work or ever said ‘thank you.’ And every year, Eleanor would tell her it was not enough, and Kate would be punished.

    This year felt very different. Kate still made sure to wake up early. Because Tasha ACTUALLY deserves this. Earlier this week, Kate had Wanda take her to the store to get Natasha some gifts, and to teach her how to make French Toast using Natasha’s favorite sandwich: Peanut Butter and Jelly.

    At 5:30 a.m., she got to work.

    She made sure to ask FRIDAY to soundproof the kitchen, so the sounds would not wake Natasha up early as she worked diligently at the stove. Once the French Toast was done, she ate the first sandwich she accidently burnt, cleaned all the dishes, and set up all of Natasha’s gifts.

    Smiling once she had everything set up the way she had pictured in her head, Kate looks up to see that it is 6:25 a.m. Perfect. She calls for Lucky, and quietly leaves the apartment to take him on a walk before going to the gym for a while.

    Natasha groans when her alarm goes off at 6:30 a.m. She did not sleep well at all the night before. Not only is Wanda gone on a mission and won’t be home til later, but something about Mother’s Day has always bothered her. In years past, it made her angry because she thought that her mother had abandoned her. However, this year after Melina told her that her mother did not really want to give her up and even tried to get her back, her usual anger has been replaced by a subtle sorrow and restlessness about the mother she has lost. And after tossing and turning all night, she just wants to go back to bed. Damn, I really wish Wanda was here to snuggle or Yelena was here to spar with. (Yelena was back in Russia for the week).

    Sighing, she gets out of bed anyway, changes into her work out clothes, and heads out to the kitchen to get her coffee before hitting the gym.

    Her breath catches and she pauses as she notices the items laid out so precisely on the kitchen counter.

    A plate of fancy french toast, a cup of orange juice, a mug of coffee, a gift bag, a vase filled with delicate flowers, and a card, leaning against the gift bag, that has ‘Tasha’ written on it in Kate’s handwriting.

    This is… for me?

    That warm feeling comes back with a vengeance.

    The corners of her mouth tug up into a small smile and she feels tears start to spring into her eyes.

    She slowly walks over to it, as if it might explode or as if it is a sick dream her mind has conjured up, and takes a seat in her usual stool.

    Due to her conflicting feelings earlier this morning, she had not planned on eating breakfast. However, as she got closer and closer to the plate, and especially when she noticed it was still warm, all of her previous plans flew out of the window, and she begins to eat.

    Damn, where the hell did Kate learn to cook like this? And how is it still warm?

    Once she was done eating, she decides to start with the card first, and opens the envelope. The front of the card, in huge capital letters, says ‘HAPPY MOTHER’S DAY!’

    Her world pauses.

    Much like when Kate had deliriously called her ‘Mama’ when she was really sick, the words on the card shake her to her core. She doesn’t know why she didn’t realize sooner that this is why Kate did all of this.

    She is wishing…me?…a happy mother’s day?

    The love that Natasha feels for Kate bursts through her, and it is almost too much.

    And I haven’t even read the inside of the card yet.

    Inside the card, there is a little note written in Kate’s slightly messy handwriting:

    ‘Tasha – Thank you for everything that you have done for me. You didn’t have to take me in, but you did, and honestly it was the best thing that has ever happened to me. For most of my life, I hated this holiday because I spent it “celebrating” a woman who hardly deserved it. But this year, and every year from now on, I am so happy that I finally have someone I am proud and thankful to celebrate and who deserves it. Thank you so much for taking care of me, being patient with me, and, most of all, loving me. I am so proud and lucky to have you in my life. Happy Mother’s Day.

    Love, Tvoy Malen’kiy Yastreb p.s. if you liked the french toast, I made it. If you didn’t, Wanda did ;)’

    Natasha hadn’t even noticed the tears streaming down her cheeks until she finished reading. Chuckling at the last bit, she reached up to wipe the tears off.

    She set her card down in front of the vase of flowers, then reached for the gift bag. She did too much, she didn’t need to do any of this. This kid. She smiles.

    She opens the bag to find a new sleek dark purple knife, because in Kate’s logic what else would you get a Black Widow?; a mug, that Kate had clearly painted at a pottery place, that had ‘Tasha’s Mug’ painted on one side with her widow symbol on the other and a hand flipping the middle finger on the bottom of the mug; and finally, a new hoodie to replace the one Kate had officially stolen from her months ago.

    She takes a picture of her new things, along with her card and flowers, and sends them in a group chat she has with Wanda and Laura.

    Natasha concludes that this might be the happiest she has ever felt in her life thus far. Damn, this kid really knows how to make me feel better without even trying.

    Kate returns to the apartment around 8:00 to find Natasha on the couch watching TV, wearing her new hoodie and using her new mug. Kate smiles. Lucky bounds over to Natasha eager to get pats from his other favorite human, and that is when Natasha notices Kate has returned. “Good morning!” Kate greets brightly, Natasha gets up from the couch and makes her way over to Kate, “I was down at the gym, I was kinda surprised that you didn’t come down to work out—oof,”

    Natasha pulls Kate into a very tight hug, that Kate, after some initial shock, returns enthusiastically. She is surprised when Natasha starts to take some shuddering breaths and to sniffle.

    “Thank you malyshka,” Natasha says sincerely, “I am very thankful and proud to have you in my life too.”

    Kate leans harder into Natasha’s embrace and tucks her head under her chin.

    “Happy Mother’s Day, Tasha,” Kate replies, just as sincerely. Natasha shifts slightly out of the embrace to wipe away her tears, kisses Kate on the forehead, then she takes Kate by the hand to the couch for a cuddle.

    After a few moments of comfortable silence, Natasha speaks up softly, “Mother’s Day has always been hard for me. My whole life, I believed that my mother abandoned me on the street as a baby. That is what they always told me. But,” She pauses and Kate takes her hand and squeezes it, letting her know that she is listening, “after my mission with Yelena a month ago, I had found out that my mother was forced to let me go because my family was compensated for me. She even tried to find me, but Dreykov had her killed.”

    A tear runs down Natasha’s cheek, and Kate leans further into Natasha and gives her a tight hug.

    “And this morning, I was feeling a little sad and confused. But when I saw all this,” Natasha gestures about and looks down at Kate, “I instantly started to feel better. When I finished eating and opening up my gifts, I felt so light and happy. Thank you moy malen’kiy yastreb.” She places a kiss on her hairline. “I love you.”

    “I love you too, Tasha.”

    They continued to cuddle on the couch and watch TV, and since Kate woke up so early and Natasha had barely slept the night before, they both soon fell asleep.

    Wanda walks into a very quiet apartment a few hours later. She sees that the TV is still on but is showing a screensaver, as if someone didn’t hit the ‘Continue Playing Without Asking Again’ button on Netflix. Putting down her bag, she walks over to the couch to see Kate and Natasha snuggling into the couch, with Lucky at their feet, all of them fast asleep.

    Smiling, she pulls the blanket over the back of the couch over them gently, and heads to take a shower.

    After her shower, she finds the trio still asleep. Noticing it is a little after 1:00, she heads into the kitchen to make lunch. I wonder if they ate lunch yet?

    As she starts to make a quick paprikash, she hears soft rustling followed by soft footsteps and looks up to see that Natasha is awake. She smiles at her while standing at the stove.

    “Hi detka,” Wanda says softly.

    “Hello moya lyubov,” Natasha replies, wrapping her arms around Wanda’s waist from behind and giving her a quick kiss.

    “How was your morning?” Wanda asks, leaning back into Natasha’s embrace.

    “Perfect,” Natasha hums, smiling and not moving from her spot and hooking her chin over Wanda’s shoulder, “just perfect.”

    “I’m glad.” Wanda smiles again.

    “So... whatcha making?”

    “Haha, I take it you guys didn’t eat lunch?” Wanda laughs softly.

    “No, we had breakfast. Or at least I had breakfast. I don’t know if Kate ate. I hope she did… Anyways, after she got back from taking Lucky on an early walk, we just fell asleep. We have been asleep ever since,” Natasha ends with a chuckle.

    “In that case; since you got up, and I started making this, I give it 5 seconds.”

    “5 seconds for what?”

    Wanda doesn’t answer, and just holds up her hand, putting down a finger every time a second goes by, “5… 4… 3… 2… 1…” then she points to the couch to where Kate starts to slowly move about, waking up.

    Kate gets up and walks into the kitchen rubbing her eye.

    She looks at Natasha first, not seeming to notice that Wanda was home, “You left,” she whines with what Natasha and Wanda both deem an adorable pout.

    “I’m sorry, malen’kiy yastreb, but I wanted to say hello to Wanda,” Natasha says.

    Then Kate realizes that Wanda is home.

    “Wands! You are home!”

    “Haha, hi Katie-bug!” Wanda laughs and moves around Natasha to give Kate a hug, “I hope you are hungry because I am making your favorite.”

    “Oh my god yes! I haven’t eaten since 6:00 this morning,” Kate complains playfully as she sits on a stool at the kitchen island, “I am starving!”

    Natasha and Wanda laugh.

    The rest of the day, Kate was determined to fill with Natasha’s favorite activities. Her and Natasha went down to the gym after lunch, and Natasha even let Kate shoot a gun with her at the shooting range. After, Wanda met up with them to do some yoga and meditation. Well, Kate tried to mediate, but she could not sit still long enough, which ended up giving everyone a case of the giggles, and Natasha wouldn’t’ve had it any other way. They ordered food from Natasha’s favorite take-out place, and had a picnic on the roof of the Compound, watching the stars. They ended the night watching Natasha’s favorite James Bond film, Moonraker.

    At the end of the movie, they all decided it was time for bed.

    As she was getting ready for bed, Kate turned to see Natasha standing in her doorway. Kate sends her a smile. Natasha returns it, walking in, and nods for Kate to get into bed.

    Kate gets into bed and gets all comfy. Natasha sits on the side of the bed facing her, “I just wanted to say thank you for today,” Natasha says softly.

    “Tasha,” Kate replies playfully rolling her eyes with a smirk, “You know that the whole point of today was for me to thank you, right?”

    Natasha chuckles, “Seriously, dorogoy. Today was amazing.”

    “Well get used to it,” Kate smiles, “Because I am going even bigger next year!”

    They both laugh.

    “I mean it, Tasha,” Kate says sincerely, “I am so thankful for you. Today was just a little itty- bitty thing compared to what you have done and do for me every day,” Kate pauses and looks down, “I used to think the way Eleanor parented me was the way that I deserved to be treated.” She looks back up at Natasha, “You showed me that I was wrong; that she was wrong. You showed me what it is like to have a parent that actually cares and loves you. A real parent. And I am forever grateful for that. I am forever grateful for you.”

    Natasha just smiles and pulls Kate into a hug, “it was all just instinct, malen’kiy yastreb. You have the biggest heart. And you are the best child a parent could ever ask for. I am forever grateful for you too.”

    “I love you, Tasha.”

    “I love you too malyshka.”

    also: tvoy malen'kiy yastreb = your little hawk Whistleblower Chapter Notes TW for blood and panic attack See the end of the chapter for more notes A few days after the archery championship, in which Midtown took home the gold and Kate came in first of course, Natasha and Wanda both had to leave for a mission. Leaving Kate without either of them for the first time.

    Alone.

    Well not alone.

    Yelena was staying with her. And this made Natasha slightly nervous.

    Not that she didn’t trust Yelena, because she trusted her with her life and for her to keep Kate safe while they were gone. Mostly because whenever Kate and Yelena were together, they seemed to cause… trouble.

    Pranks on Bucky and Sam, making a lot of noise throughout the Compound at inconvenient hours of the day, constantly hiding in the vents, and even somehow breaking lights on the training room ceiling.

    Needless to say, Natasha was a little wary of leaving Kate and her sister alone without a proper adult.

    She was also worried because Yelena had never had to help Kate through a nightmare or panic attack. Even though Kate’s nightmares have become very infrequent and she hadn’t had a panic attack in months, Natasha was worried that if she had one, Kate wouldn’t seek Yelena out for help like she does with her and now Wanda. That Kate would just hold everything in and pretend that she is fine.

    So, before they left, Natasha had a quick conversation with Yelena.

    “Make sure to check the soundproofing settings on her room before you go to bed every night. She has used that before,” Natasha says.

    “Okay,” Yelena replies, nodding along.

    “And if she does wake up from a nightmare, sometimes it takes a few minutes for her to realize where she is, so make sure to reassure her that she is safe,” Natasha lists.

    “Mm-hm,” Yelena nods.

    “Make sure to reassure her right away or she might start to have a panic attack,” Natasha adds, “The first signs of a panic attack are uneven and fast breathing,”

    “I know, Natasha.”

    “And, hopefully she doesn’t, but if she starts to get a panic attack, the 5-4-3-2-1 method works the best to help calm her down. And—”

    “Natka,” Yelena stops her, “You guys will only be gone for three days. I think she will be fine. And if she is not, you do remember that I basically help other widows not have panic attacks for a living, right?”

    Natasha chuckles softly, “I know. I am sorry. This is just the first time Kate won’t be around the both of us since everything happened. I am just a little worried is all.”

    “I know,” Yelena sends her a soft smile, “Kate is going to be just fine. And you know that I will protect her with my life if I need to.”

    Natasha hugs Yelena, “Spasibo, mladshaya sestra.”

    After a beat, Natasha pulls back, “But no causing trouble and not too much junk food.”

    Kate wasn’t gonna lie. She had initially been nervous when she had learned that both Wanda and Natasha were needed for the mission. Of course, she loves and trusts Yelena, but she didn’t know if she could be open with her yet if she was feeling sad or depressed or had a nightmare.

    But these three days have passed by pretty fast. And Kate had yet to have had a nightmare or have felt uneasy for an extended period of time. She wasn’t missing them like she thought she would’ve.

On Sunday, Yelena had taken Kate out clothes shopping and to the movies to see the Little Mermaid remake, of course buying all the snacks and candy Kate had wanted. After school on Monday, they binged all of the Star Wars movies after Yelena had said she hadn’t seen them. And on Tuesday, she even taught Kate how to make her famous homemade mac ‘n’ cheese, which has been upgraded from Kraft, thank you, for Family Dinner.

    By the time dinner was over, everyone decided to watch some TV in the common room as Tony and Pepper had stopped by with Morgan and since they knew that Natasha and Wanda would be back soon.

    “Does anyone else want dessert? I think I am going to make myself a bowl of ice cream,”

    Kate asks the group. After a chorus of different variations of ‘no thank you, I am full,’ Kate made her way back into the kitchen.

    “So, Yelena,” Sam turns to her once Kate is out of earshot, “How has Kate been while Natasha and Wanda have been gone?”

    “Honestly, pretty good,” Yelena smiles, “I don’t know if I have just been keeping her distracted pretty well or if she is just in a better mental space than the last time Natasha had a mission.”

    “Yeah, I have noticed that she is more upbeat this week,” Steve adds.

    “Maybe you will have to come around the next time Natasha has a mission,” Bruce suggests. “I will say I am very glad, and proud, that you two decided not to pull any pranks on me while Nat and Wanda are away,” Bucky sighs in relief.

    “Who says we didn’t?” Yelena replies mischievously, and Bucky’s eyes go wide, then she chuckles, “Oh I am just joking Zimniy Soldat, calm down.”

    “Tony, can you just pick something already? It’s not like we are really going to watch it,” Pepper asks, getting annoyed.

    “Yeah seriously, Tone, all the clicking is starting to ruin the vibe,” Bruce adds.

    “Fine, if you guys want me to stop, I will stop,” Tony grumbles, stopping his search and just letting the TV play on a random station.

    What everyone didn’t know is that this station was the news station, as it was on a commercial when Tony landed on it. The commercials end, and before they can change the channel, loud theme music is playing, and an anchor is reporting.

    ‘BREAKING NEWS: New information from the Eleanor Bishop trial was released to the public today after a press whistleblower broke a non-disclosure agreement and leaked classified documents to the public on social media. The documents contain information on why Bishop received a longer than usual sentence for her participation in the murder of Armand Duquesne III. The documents revealed that she had been emotionally and physically abusing her daughter, Katherine Bishop, for over seven years—"

    The sound of a bowl shattering on the ground made everyone turn to see a shocked Kate standing behind the couches.

    Kate’s heartrate immediately started to accelerate once she saw her mother’s face on TV, but by the time they said her own name, she thought it was going to beat out of her chest. When she finally tore her eyes from the screen, she saw everyone looking at her with concern.

    “I’m s-sorry. I-I will c-clean this up,” Kate stutters as she crouches down and starts to pick up the broken pieces of the glass bowl.

    Yelena immediately gets up and crouches down next to Kate.

    “It is okay, malen'kaya ptichka,” Yelena says softly, “We can clean this up later. Are you okay?”

    Yelena looks at Kate, who’s eyes suggest that her mind is far away.

    “Kate?” she tries to get her attention again, and fails. Then she looks down to see that the pieces of the bowl have cut Kate’s hands. Kate doesn’t seem to notice as she continues to pick up pieces.

    Yelena then puts her hand on Kate’s shoulder. This breaks Kate out of her trance and she looks back up at Yelena who asks her again, “Are you okay, Kate?”

    Kate looks down and sees blood on her hands. She starts to breath faster and get more panicked.

    Oh my gosh, what happened? Everyone knows.

    She looks up to see everyone still looking at her with pained expressions of concern.

    Oh my god. They know. They all know. Everyone knows. What are they going to think of me now? That I deserved it? That I was a weakling for not being able to fight her off? That I am the useless piece of garbage Eleanor made me out to me? Oh my god. The whole world knows.

    Kate immediately gets up and darts off down the hallways of the Compound, not hearing everyone’s shouts for her.

    “FRIDAY, don’t let them know where I am going,” she asks the AI, not even listening to the response.

    She debates on where to go. She doesn’t want to be found; she wants to be alone. After a few minutes of running up and down corridors, she finds a small supply closet near one of the empty apartments, and hides.

    As soon as she sits on the floor of the closet, she lets out the sobs she has been holding.

    “FRIDAY, where did she go?” Tony asks the AI immediately once Yelena comes back empty handed from their apartment.

    “I am not sure, Boss. Kate had asked me not to track her,” The AI responds.

    “Well, override her request,” Tony requests.

    “I am sorry, Boss, but you do not have the authority to override this type of request. Agent Romanoff and Agent Maximoff are currently the only two people who have this ability under the ‘Mama Bear Protocol’ you set up a few months ago,” FRIDAY says.

    Tony groans, “Damn me and my brilliant brain!!”

    “So that’s it?” Pepper asks, “FRIDAY can’t find her?”

    “Nope,” Tony replies, “I suggest we all split up and find her. And after we make sure she is okay, come up with a plan to murder Eleanor Bishop in prison without having it traced back to us.”

    “Tony—” Pepper starts.

    “I’m serious!” Tony exclaims.

    “Good luck, brother,” Sam sends Steve a sympathetic look.

While searching, he gets a text from Natasha telling him that they have arrived, and will start looking in places where Kate has hidden before. Finding the apartment empty, Steve continues down the hall.

    He pauses when he starts to hear faint whimpers. Following the noise leads him to a small storage closet. He takes his phone out and texts Yelena, Natasha, and Wanda where he is and that she has found her. Then sends a message to the team telling them he found her.

    Yelena shows up first, and immediately goes to the door. Steve stays outside for privacy. She slowly opens the door to the closet finding Kate crying on the floor with her hands over her ears, breathing heavily.

    My poor plemyannitsa. Natka told me she does this when she is very upset.

    She slowly sinks down to the floor and sits across from Kate. She can see that there is dried blood from her hands all over her shirt and on the sides of her face from where she is covering her ears.

    “Kate?” she tries softly. When the teen shows no sign of seeing or hearing her, she very delicately takes Kate’s hands away from her ears, “Are you okay, malen’kaya ptichka?”

    Kate shakes her head no and her tears become heavier. Her breathing also becomes more uneven. Yelena’s heart breaks.

    “Can you try to take a deep breath for me, Kate?” Yelena asks calmly, “Once we regulate your breathing, you will start to feel better.” She watches as Kate tries and miserably fails to get in a deep breath, so she moves behind her and pulls her against her chest, “Follow my breathing, malen’kaya ptichka.”

    Kate tries and tries, but she still can’t get her breathing under control, and she starts to panic more. More tears start to roll down her face as she starts to claw at the neck of her shirt. “Shhh, shhh, Kate. It is okay, you are safe,” Yelena soothes, as she tries to calm Kate’s hands away from her shirt, “You are safe. Can you name five things you see?”

    Kate immediately shakes her head no, and squeezes her eyes shut. There are too many things and too many thoughts running through her mind at the moment. Yelena feels tears start to prick her own eyes as she feels like she is out of options to help her niece.

    Then, Natasha and Wanda appear in the doorway, still in their mission clothes; their hearts breaking at the sight. Natasha moves to sit in front of Kate, while Wanda sits to the side. “Malyshka?” Natasha asks.

    At this Kate opens her eyes, seeing her favorite person in front of her starts to help slow the racing thoughts in her brain. Natasha reaches out and takes Kate’s hands from, Yelena who immediately wraps her arms around Kate in a protective embrace. Wanda puts her hand on Kate’s shoulder and slowly rubs soothing circles onto it.

    “It's okay dorogoy, you are safe. I need you to name five things that you can see, baby,” Natasha says softly but firmly as she moves one hand up to caress Kate's cheek.

    “Y-you, W-wanda, Yelena’s b-boots, y-your necklace, and th-the door.”

    “Good job, malyshka. Four things you can feel?”

    “Y-yelena breathing, y-your hands, the c-carpet, and my clothes.”

    “You are doing great, malen’kiy yastreb. Three things you can hear?”

    “M-my breathing, you, and the blood r-rushing in m-my ears.”

    “Almost there, dorogoy. Two things you can smell?”

    “Mac ‘n’ cheese, and vanilla.”

    “Last one, what is one thing you can taste?”

    “Salt.” From my tears.

    Kate slumps back against Yelena, exhausted, her breathing back to normal as tears still fall rapidly down her cheeks. Then she reaches out for Natasha who immediately pulls her into her arms. Wanda moves closer to rub soothing circles on her back, and Yelena does the same. “Oh baby,” Natasha soothes, “It’s going to be okay. You are okay. You are safe.”

    “They all know… everyone knows,” Kate says softly, her voice raw. She pushes her face into Natasha’s neck to hide from the world.

    After a few minutes, and Kate’s tears slow, Natasha picks her up in her arms, and they all head back to the apartment.

    Sitting down on the couch, Natasha pulls Kate to sit on her lap sideways so that they can look at her hands and clean her up a bit. Wanda comes over with a damp face cloth and a new shirt, while Yelena hunts around the first aid kit for supplies.

    “Can Wanda look at your hands and wash your face a bit, malyshka?” Natasha asks her. Kate doesn’t pick her head up off of Natasha’s chest, but nods anyways. Wanda makes quick work of cleaning the dried blood off of Kate’s face and hands before helping Kate change into a clean t-shirt.

    Then Yelena comes over with her supplies and checks over Kate’s hands, “Looks like these don’t need any stitches. Just to be cleaned and bandaged,” She informs them, then does just that. Once she is done, she sits next to Kate, and Wanda sits next to Natasha, and they all sit in comfortable silence.

    “I promise, malyshka.”

    Natasha and Wanda smile.

    Then Natasha’s phone starts to ring, the caller ID showing that Clint is calling.

    “Thank you for looking after her, mladshaya sestra.”

    Natasha kisses her hairline.

    “Goodnight, moya malen’kiy yastreb.”

    Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes The day after the whistleblower made Kate’s past public, Natasha called the school to let them know that Kate would be out for the rest of the week, which Kate was grateful for.     And I am definitely not ready to see the therapist next week.

    In the days following the incident, it was clear to Natasha that Kate was not doing well. She hardly spoke unless it was necessary, she had multiple nightmares a night, and even had another panic attack after a really bad one. Her poor girl was exhausted. Most of the day she spent curled up next to either her, Wanda, or Yelena, and she would visibly tense if anyone else came around to check up on them. It made her blood boil.

    So, when Kate was sleeping, they got to work.

    The boys came over and they started their search for the greasy little rat that leaked the information. Upon finding he was hiding out somewhere in the rural parts of northern Vermont, Steve, Bucky, and Sam left to go bring him in.

    They brought him back to the Compound on Thursday; Natasha, Wanda, and Yelena had a very long conversation, if you will, with the man before they called the authorities to bring him into custody.

    It helped to calm the anger in her veins, but not completely absolve it.

    So she when she woke up, she gently slipped out of Natasha’s arms, as she had been spending most nights with Kate because of the nightmares, and headed down to the archery range. She was honestly surprised when Natasha did not wake up when she was getting up.

    She must be really tired. I feel so bad, its my fault she has been up most of the previous nights.

    Archery has always had a calming effect on Kate. It would always simplify all her thoughts; it was almost like meditation for her. And in times like this, it would pull her focus so much she would often not even notice her surroundings.

    Like Peter walking into the range, and touching her shoulder.

    “JESUS Christ!” Kate exclaims, whipping around to face him, “Peter! You scared the shit outta me!”

    “Sorry, Katie!” Peter apologizes.

    “Wait. What are you doing here? It’s Friday. Shouldn’t you be in school?” She asks him.

    “Technically, yes,” Peter chuckles sending her a soft smile, “But I took it off to see how my best friend was doing.”

    Kate’s heart automatically feels warmer. She laughs then gives Peter a hug.

    “Thank you,” She replies softly. Then she jokes, “Don’t tell Ned you called me your best friend, he will get jealous.”

    Peter laughs.

    They spend the rest of the morning and early afternoon making jokes and playing MarioKart. Having Peter over was doing wonders for Kate’s mood. When Natasha comes back into the apartment after a meeting, she smiles when she hears laughter.

    “Hey guys! What are ya up to?” She asks, coming to sit on the couch next to Kate.

    “Hi Tasha!” Kate smiles, “We are just finishing up a round of MarioKart. You want in next round?”

    “I would love to, but I think you and Peter have some other plans,” Natasha replies with a smirk.

    Kate then looks between Natasha and Peter with a slightly confused expression.

    “We don’t have any other plans, right Peter?” Kate asks.

    “Actually Katie,” Peter says looking down at his phone checking the time, “We do!”

    “Oh-kay,” Kate says slowly, “anyone care to fill me in on these plans that apparently involve me that I do not know anything about?”

    Peter looks at Natasha, and Natasha looks to Kate with a smile.

    “Well, I have been in contact with Peter,” Natasha explains, “who has talked with Greer and Franny. I have completed their background checks, and I think it would be nice to finally meet them and have them over for dinner. So, I asked Peter to invite them on your behalf. You guys will leave in a few minutes to pick them up and come back here. What do you say?”

    “Wait, really?” Kate smiles.

    “Of course, malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha says softly cupping one of Kate’s cheeks with her hand, “I know this week has been hard for you. And I am so proud of you for making it this far.”

    Kate leans into Natasha’s palm, “Thank you, Tasha.”

    Natasha leans in and kisses Kate’s temple, “I love you malyshka. Now why don’t you go get ready, you guys should probably leave soon.”

    Kate jumps up and runs to her room to change.

    As Peter drove them to the school, Kate started to feel anxious. She was excited to see her friends after the shitshow that was the beginning of the week, but on the same hand, she was worried that all this would alter their view of her. Yes, they had been texting with her all week to see how she was and to let her know that they were there for her, but did they really mean it? AND not to mention, she was going to be telling them that she was adopted by a LITERAL Avenger, lives at the Avenger’s Compound, AND that they would be having dinner with said Avenger(s).

    Pulling up to the school, Kate tries her hardest to shove all her anxiety down before her friends see her. The final bell rings and students pour out of the front doors. Within a few minutes, Kate and Peter spot Ned, Mj, Greer, and Franny walking towards Peter’s car.

    “Hi guys,” Kate waves.

    “Kate!” Franny and Greer yell in unison, then almost tackle Kate in a hug.

    “Easy, you two. You are gonna squeeze all the air out of me,” Kate laughs hugging them back.

    “Hey Kate!” Mj greets, giving Kate a quick hug, “You missed Ned’s big blunder yesterday at lunch.”

    “It was not a blunder!” Ned protests, “I just may have accidently tried to throw away my milk carton from far away and it may have landed all over Mr. Prescott.”

    Everyone laughs.

    “Damn!” Kate says still laughing, “Did you get it on video?”

    “Unfortunately, no,” Mj sighs.

    “Alright, alright,” Ned interjects, “If you guys are done laughing, I gotta go. I promised to take my Lola shopping tonight.”

    “I gotta go too, Nero’s coffee doesn’t sell itself. Even though I wish it would. See you later, guys,” Mj says giving Kate a quick hug, then she turns to Peter and gives him a quick kiss, “Bye Pete. Text me later.”

    “Okay!” Peter says clapping his hands once, “Lets get this show on the road.”

    They all pile into Peter’s car, and they start on the way to the Compound. They talk for most of the way. As they get closer, Kate tries to find a way to tell them where they are going without just blurting it out.

    “We are so happy that Peter, and I guess inadvertently your adoptive parents, invited us to come over today,” Franny says.

    “Yeah!” Greer agrees, “I didn’t even know you and Peter were so close. Are you guys related or something?”

    “Not technically,” Kate replies, “We—”

    “She is basically my little sister,” Peter sends Kate a smile, “We met through Mr. Stark around New Years and have been best friends ever since.”

    Kate smiles back.

    “Wait, Kate you know Tony Stark?” Franny asks.

    “Um, yeah,” Kate says rubbing the back of her neck, looking out the window, she realizes they are going to be pulling up to the Compound any second, “I actually have something to tell you guys—”

    “Woah!” Franny and Greer gasp, as they pull up to the Compound.

    “I thought you said that we were going to your house?” Franny asks.

    “We are,” Kate replies, “I, um, live here.”

    “You LIVE here?” Greer asks dumbfounded.

    “Uh yeah,” Kate chuckles.

    Peter pulls into his parking spot and then turns to the group as they get out of the car, “Alright guys, I will see you later. When Mr. Stark calls, I answer. An intern’s work is never done.” “Bye Peter!” The group calls.

    Kate leads them through the Compound, slightly chuckling at every ‘ooo’ and ‘oh my god’ as they walk down the halls. Then they reach the door to the apartment and Kate pauses before opening the door, turning around to face her friends.

    I should probably tell them before we get into the apartment in case Tasha is still in there. It’s now or never, I guess.

    “So, as I was saying earlier, I do live here. After all the craziness at Christmas, which I will fully explain once we get into the apartment, I was going to be placed in foster care. Natasha and Clint, who both helped me during the said Christmas debacle, did not like that idea, so Natasha decided to be my emergency foster parent. Then Eleanor goes to jail, loses her parental rights, and bam, Natasha adopts me. Now I officially live here in an apartment with her, my dog, her sister, and Wanda, and now, my real name is Katherine Elizabeth Romanoff,” Kate explains rapidly without taking a breath, “So yeah. Whew,” Kate takes a quick breath, “Any questions?”

    Franny and Greer look at her in shock with their eyes wide and their mouths open.

    “So. So many,” Franny just replies.

    Kate laughs then opens the door. Lucky immediately runs over to greet her and to sniff Franny and Greer.

    “This is Lucky!” Kate smiles as she crouches down to give him pats, “I saved him by running into traffic.”

    Franny and Greer instantly also crouch down to give the happy dog some love.

    “We sometimes call him Pizza Dog because he loves to eat Pizza,” Kate says, then she whispers, “I usually sneak him some when Tasha isn’t looking.”

    “You may think you are sneaking it, but I see you do it every time, malen’kiy yastreb.”

    The teens heads all snap up to see Natasha walking over from the living room.

    “Are you sure you have caught me every time?” Kate smirks. Natasha laughs. The girls stand up, “Tasha, this is Franny and Greer, Franny and Greer this is Natasha.”

    Natasha shakes their hands.

    “It is very nice to meet you girls. Kate talks about you guys non-stop,” Natasha says sending them a smile.

    “It is so nice to meet you,” Franny replies, “I still can’t believe that Kate lives here with you.” Natasha and Kate chuckle.

    “Well, I will let you guys hang out. I gotta run down to the gym; I promised Yelena I would go spar after I finished up with some paperwork,” Natasha says while reaching to give Kate a hug and a quick kiss to her temple, then she looks down at Kate talking softly, “If you need me, just call me and I will be here right away, okay dorogoy?”

    “Okay,” Kate says returning the hug, “Thanks Tasha.”

    And with that, Natasha leaves the apartment and the teens move over to the kitchen to grab a snack.

    “Wow,” Greer states, still shocked, “I can’t believe that we just met The Black Widow.”

    “Yup,” Kate laughs softly, “that was my life around Christmas.”

    “So,” Franny says, “What exactly happened during Christmas that led to all this?”

    “Alright guys, buckle in because it is one long, crazy story. So it all started at a charity event Eleanor, my biological mother, made me attend…”

    After a long retelling of the events of the past holidays and all the shit surrounding Eleanor’s trial followed by many questions, Kate felt a weight lift off of her shoulders. Her friends now knew the truth about her past, and her present, and they both seemed to be taking it in stride. Well, stride and a little fangirling. She definitely felt better than she did earlier in the week. She showed her friends her room, and they played on Kate’s Nintendo Switch (one that Tony had bought for her this earlier this week) while gossiping about the things Kate had missed at school that week.

    Around 5:00, Natasha walked back into the apartment with Wanda and Yelena in tow, carrying Kate’s favorite pizza. Upon hearing giggles and laughter coming from Kate’s room Wanda and Yelena turned to Natasha with smiles.

    “She sounds so happy,” Wanda says softly, still smiling.

    “I know,” Natasha replies, “I didn’t know how much I really missed hearing her laugh until I heard her laughing earlier with Peter.”

    Then Lucky bounds out of Kate’s room, definitely because he could smell the pizza, and lets out a happy bark to see the rest of his pack home with his favorite snack. Kate, Franny, and Greer follow.

    “Ooo! You got Herman’s!” Kate says excitedly pointing to the pizza box in Natasha’s hands. “Yup!” Wanda replies, walking over to give Kate a hug, “I know it’s your favorite!”

    “Thank you, Wands,” Kate returns the hug, then pulls back, “Wanda this is Franny and Greer, my best friends. Franny and Greer this is Wanda, and over there, helping Tasha get out the plates, is my Aunt Yelena.”

    “It is so nice to meet you,” Franny smiles.

    “Likewise!” Wanda says, “Kate has told us so much about you guys, I have been dying to meet her best friends.”

    Kate blushes slightly, and Greer nudges her, “Aww! You talk about us?”

    “Shut up,” Kate jokes, laughing.

    “Okay kids,” Natasha laughs along, “It is time for dinner.”

    The rest of the night was filled with laughter and warmth. Kate went with Natasha and Wanda to drive Franny and Greer home, making sure to hug them tight and thank them for helping making her feel better as they dropped them off. They also explained to them the importance of keeping Kate’s homelife/real identity a secret, and they both promised not to tell a single soul.

    Back at the Compound, Kate curled up in her usual spot next to/on top of Natasha while they all watched the final season of She-ra.

    “We are going to need to find a new show soon, there are only seven episodes left,” Natasha says.

    “Mm-hm,” Kate hums, snuggling further into Natasha’s embrace. Natasha looks down at Kate.

    “Are you falling asleep on me, malen’kiy yastreb?” Natasha smirks.

    “No, no I am not.” Kate replies, trying and failing to seem more awake.

    “It’s okay, this can be our last episode tonight. Then we can head to bed,” Wanda suggests. “That works, I am exhausted after chasing that stupid robot all day in training,” Yelena sighs. “He isn’t really a robot, Lena,” Natasha supplies.

    “I don’t care,” Yelena grumbles, “He made me run around the whole facilities today because he thought that training should require it.”

    Kate chuckles softly.

    After a moment, she looks up at Natasha, “Tasha?”

    “Yes, dorogoy?”

    “Thank you for today,” Kate says, “I feel a lot better than I did earlier this week.”

    “I am glad,” Natasha replies, “I love you malyshka.”

    “Love you too, Tasha.”

    Sure, Kate was scared for the week to come. Dealing with the unwanted looks of pity and rumors at school and having her first therapy appointment. But now that her friends knew everything, and with her chosen family behind her, she knew that she would be able to get through it.

    She cuddles further into Natasha, and sure enough, is asleep within seconds.

But after a few meetings, Kate had started to feel more comfortable and no longer needed Natasha to stay. Peter, Ned, and Mj have another final today so I was thinking we could hang in the library while we wait for them to be done.”

    Many times, actually.”

    “Aw come on, dude,” Kate says as her and Franny each put an arm around Greer and lead her to homeroom, “Just think, in two short hours, we will be on summer break.”

    “Me too!” Greer adds.

    “See, that is why we are all good friends!” Greer says as they walk into the library, “Kate is really smart when it comes to the Math and Science stuff and you are really great with the English and History. And I suck at it all, and you both help me!”

    They laugh as they find a table and put their bags down on top of it as they each take a seat. “So Kate, what are your plans for this summer?” Franny asks her, “Outside of taking some much-needed rest of course.”

    “Well, I am definitely planning on doing that,” she chuckles, “Other than that, we are planning a few trips to visit Clint and his family, possibly making one of them a road trip. More training with Tasha; I am hoping she finally teaches me that cool body throw thing she does with her legs. And… OH! We might be planning a trip to go to Russia to visit Tasha and Yelena’s parents.”

    “That’s awesome!” Greer says, “and I am just planning on begging my parents to take me up to the lake a few times. You guys will need to take a trip up with me this summer!”

    “Yeah! I wanna try tubbing again,” Franny excitedly cheers as she turns to Kate, “A few years ago, I tried, but my arms were too weak to hold on and I ended up getting soo much water up my nose!”

    “Oh my god!” Kate laughs, “Somehow, that doesn’t surprise me.”

    “Hey!” Franny protests.

    “Oh come on Franny, we all know you are the least athletically inclined person to exist,” Greer says while laughing.

    “I know,” Franny reluctantly agrees, then starts to chuckle.

And despite having talked about it so many times by now, Franny and Greer (mostly Greer) ask Kate about what living with Avengers is like. After telling them the story about how she convinced Clint to make a boomerang arrow, IT DID WORK despite what the old man claims, and they subsequently blew up the lab; Greer looks up at the clock.

    “Do we know how much longer they will be?” Greer asks.

    “Probably not too much longer,” Kate says, “Peter told me that they will probably take the whole class time. I guess their Chem final had a lot of material on it.”

    “Oof! That sucks,” Franny winces, “I am NOT looking forward to that class next year.”

    "Don't worry Fran," Greer responds, "We will have Kate to explain everything to us."

    “I think since they might be a little while longer, I might head down to the vending machines to grab a snack,” Kate says getting up, “Do you guys want anything?”

    “No, I am good!” Greer replies.

    “Same,” Franny smiles.

    “Alright, I will be back in a little while.” And with that, Kate heads out of the library.

    On the walk down to the cafeteria, Kate can’t help but notice that the hallways are eerily empty.

    Huh, you’d think there would be other students milling around since it is the last day of school. Something is off.

    As she walks into the empty cafeteria, Kate can’t help but feel unsettled. The hair on the back of her neck starts to stand up, and she starts to get the feeling like not everything is right. Alright, let’s just get my Strawberry Poptart and leave. No need to hang around.

    She walks up to the vending machine and gets out her money.

    Before putting it into the machine, Kate swears she hears footsteps from the corner of the cafeteria. She looks, and there is no one in sight.

    “Hello?” She tries.

    No response.

    The unsettling feeling, she had walking in, has turned into a pit in her stomach.

    I've got a bad feeling about this... You know like in Star Wars? Its in every movie?...

    Nevermind... Focus Kate, there is something off here.

    She shakes her head to clear out her thoughts and to refocus on her current task. She then quickly puts dollar into the machine and presses the code for her Poptart. The machine slowly moves its mechanisms to set the item loose.

    Come on, come on. Any day now.

    Kate glances around as she waits.

    The thunk of the Poptart hitting the bottom of the machine startles Kate. Sighing, she bends down to pick up the treat.

    As she returns to full height, looks at her reflection in the glass of the machine, and notices a large figure standing behind her.

    She goes to scream, but a large hand covers her mouth and she feels a needle prick her neck. Then her world goes black.

    So much for finally getting to relax.

    Yelena tries, speaking louder, “Natka?” when she still doesn’t get a response, “Natalia!” This makes Natasha whip her head around to face all three of them who look at her with faces of concern. Clint is the one who speaks up.

    “Nat,” he starts as calm as possible, knowing the response he will get, “I think you should get some rest.”

    “What! No! How could you even think—”

    “Detka,” Wanda tries, “Clint is right. We have been at this for 36 hours. You need to sleep. You need to eat. You need to drink something other than coffee. When we find out where Kate is, you need to be rested enough to help her.”

    “But she needs us, now! Who knows what that bitch is doing to her? We have all seen the records. I should’ve been there—"

    “No! You can stop right there,” Clint interjects standing up and walking over to Natasha, putting his hands on her shoulders, “How were we supposed to know that Eleanor escaped?

    And how would we have known she found out were Kate was. You have been doing everything you should’ve been doing. Now, Yelena and I will stay up and continue to search with FRIDAY. Go at least eat something and get some rest with Wanda. I promise we will let you know as soon as we find anything.”

    Natasha looks between him and Yelena for a few moments, before hanging her head and softly nodding. Clint brings her in to a tight hug.

    “We will find Kate. I promise you. That girl is a fighter, just like her mother,” Clint says with a sad smile, which Natasha returns, feeling tears start to spring to her eyes.

    Yelena then walks over and gives Natasha her own hug, “Birdman is right. If I know my niece, she is out there fighting. Now,” Yelena pulls out of the hug, “go get some rest sestra, before you burn yourself out.”

    Wanda takes her hand and leads her down to their apartment.

    Lucky greets them with an anxious whine, as he notices their stress and the missing member of their family. He follows them as Wanda leads Natasha over to the couch. Natasha sits down numbly, not paying attention to either Lucky or Wanda. Wanda then goes into the kitchen to make them a quick snack.

    She returns with a peanut butter and jelly sandwich and a bottle of water and tries to hand it to Natasha. When Natasha doesn’t take it, she sets it down on the coffee table, and sits next to her on the couch.

    “Detka?” Wanda calls putting her hand on Natasha’s shoulder, “Are you okay?”

    Natasha turns to her, the tears that had been welling in her eyes since leaving the conference room have finally broken free and start to roll down her face.

    “Oh detka,” Wanda says and pulls Natasha so that she can rest her head in her lap, and Natasha wraps her arms around Wanda’s torso as she starts to sob.

    “I can’t lose her Wanda,” Natasha cries into Wanda’s stomach, “she is my daughter.”

    “I know, detka, I know.” Wanda starts to card her hands through Natasha’s hair, as she feels silent tears down her own cheeks. After many tears from them both, they end up falling asleep cuddled on the couch.

    A few hours later, Wanda and Natasha are awoken by a frantic Yelena.

    “We found her.”

    They run into the conference room to find everyone already gathered. Clint pulls up a diagram of the warehouse where Kate is.

    “Based on the information FRIDAY has gathered, we believe that she is being held in this room. Arial footage shows at least 100 members of the Tracksuit Mafia standing in between the entrance and the door where Kate is. And, if this photo is to be believed,” Clint pulls up a grainy photo, “the Kingpin is also there.”

    “How? Maya told us she killed him,” Natasha asks.

    “I have no idea,” Clint says, “But we have to be ready for him. I think it is our best bet for Steve and Bucky to handle him.”

    Bucky cracks his knuckles, “Gladly.”

    “Obviously, Eleanor is also there. The authorities have asked us to bring her in alive,” Tony adds, and everyone rolls their eyes, “But who says we can’t rough her up a little?”

    “I will stay here to keep watch on everything and help Helen prep for anything she needs,” Bruce says.

    “Alright team, suit up,” Steve says stepping up, “Kate is a very important part of this family. Let’s go bring her home.”

    Each member heads off to go prepare for the upcoming mission, and Natasha heads to the locker room where she keeps her supplies.

    Natasha is quiet as she is gathering her weapons, Yelena sits down on the bench next to her. “You okay?” she asks.

    “I just hope that we aren’t too late,” Natasha says checking her Widow bites.

    “I don’t think they would still be at the warehouse if they had killed her.”

    “That’s not what I mean,” Natasha looks down, frowning, “Well it’s not entirely what I mean. I mean what if she regresses. She has made so much progress. I don’t want her to lose it. She has been through way too much in her life.”

    Yelena puts a hand on Natasha’s shoulder, prompting her to look up at her, “Like I said before: Kate is a fighter. We are going to bring her home. And it might be hard for a while, but she will have all of us behind her to support her. Most of all, you. I don’t think you realize how much she adores you, Natka. I think you just being with her will help.”

    Natasha pulls Yelena into a tight hug, “Spasibo, mladshaya sestra.”

    The next time Kate wakes up, there is frantic rustling behind the door. And she can almost hear shouts and yelling.

    What the hell? What is going on?

    Just then, the door slams open revealing a frazzled Eleanor, who quietly shuts the door, pulls Kate up to stand, which hurts like a motherfucker oh my god, puts a knife against her neck, and her hand over Kate’s mouth.

    Kate’s heart rate immediately starts to race.

    “Listen here, you little brat, and you listen closely,” Eleanor threatens as she moves around Kate, using her like a human shield, “If you make so much as a little peep, I will slit your throat right here right now, understand?”

    Kate carefully nods to show that she understands.

    They must’ve found me.

    My family.

    That is why Eleanor is so freaked.

    After what feels like hours of the sounds of gun fire, explosions, and many different objects breaking, the door slams open revealing a very angry Natasha and Wanda.

    Kate can't help but to tear up at the sight.

    They start to slowly step in the room, but they stop when they see Eleanor push the knife ever so slightly into the skin on Kate’s neck, causing Kate to whine softly behind Eleanor’s hand. “Uh, uh, uh,” Eleanor taunts, “I wouldn’t take another step if I were you. Unless you want me to hurt our poor little Kate here.”

    What the fuck happened to Eleanor in prison? I used to always think she was crazy, but something definitely broke in there.

    “Okay, okay,” Natasha says as calmly as she can holstering her gun, which was previously trained on Eleanor’s head, “just let Kate go, and we will let you walk out of here.”

    Eleanor just laughs, “Do you think I was born yesterday? As soon as I am out of that door, one of your other goons is just gonna snatch me up and take me back to Ryker's. I am not stupid. Let me and my daughter go before I have to kill her in front of you.”

    “I would seriously consider my offer,” Natasha says with venom starting to drip in her voice, “Let my daughter go now or otherwise I will let Wanda loose.”

    Wanda, who is standing off to the side, briefly lets her eyes flash red.

    “Huh,” Eleanor scoffs, looking Wanda up and down, “I am not scared of that frail thing.”

    “Well, I did warn you,” Natasha says, nodding to Wanda, who’s red magic starts to fill her eyes again.

    Within an instant Eleanor drops the knife and is on her knees with her hands over her ears, screaming. Clint comes in to haul her away, being non-too gentle either.

    As soon as Eleanor lets her out of her grip, Kate cries out and slumps forward. Natasha catches her before she hits the ground.

    “Kate?” Natasha asks, as she shifts to sit on the floor with Kate’s head in her lap. Wanda is at her side in an instant, helping Natasha cut the rope that binds her hands and feet.

    Kate just responds with another cry, squeezing her eyes shut, as the movement hurts her various injuries.

    “Malyshka? Baby?” Natasha tries again, frantically trying to get a verbal response, “Are you okay? Can you hear me? Can you open your eyes for me?”

    Kate then slowly opens her eyes and looks up to Natasha with tears in them, “Mama?”

    Mama.

    There is that word again.

    Hearing that word, that specific word, after everything that has happened in the past 36 hours, Natasha feels like she can finally breathe again.

    Tears start to flow down Natasha’s face as she carefully pulls Kate up into her arms, “It’s me, baby. You’re safe now.”

    Kate hugs her as best as she can with her one good arm, and starts to sob.

    “I was so scared, Mama,” She cries, pushing her face into Natasha’s neck to hide away from the world, “so scared.”

    “I know, baby. I know,” Natasha soothes, rubbing her hand gently on her back, “I got you now. You’re safe. Mama’s got you.”

    Recovery Chapter Notes TW for slight panic attack See the end of the chapter for more notes Once the rest of the warehouse was cleared, Natasha carried Kate to the Quinjet. The boys managed to catch Fisk; Vision, Steve, and Bucky were currently, personally, escorting him to his own very special cell at the Raft. Meanwhile, Tony and Sam were currently taking Eleanor back to prison; this time a higher security prison.

    Clint was flying the Quinjet back to the Compound while Yelena, Wanda, and Natasha were trying to coax Kate into letting them look over her injuries. However, the task was rather difficult as Kate didn't want any part of it, as she was still quite upset and did not want Natasha to put her down or even lean away from her in the slightest. Every attempt would end in Kate crying harder and shoving her face harder into Natasha’s neck to the point Natasha was worried Kate was not getting enough oxygen. Eventually, they decided to just wait until they reached the Compound, and they let Kate gradually calm down as she sat in Natasha's lap.

    By the time they made it back to the Compound, Kate had relaxed somewhat. She let Dr. Cho look over her, bandage her wounds, and cast her arm, purple of course. At the end, it was found that Kate had a broken arm, a pretty bad concussion, four broken ribs, extensive bruising along her torso, and a few cuts and bruises on her face.

    The only hindrance throughout getting checked out by Dr. Cho was the Kate had still refused to let Natasha put her down. Anytime she tried, she would instantly start to panic and get upset. Dr. Cho and everyone seemed to understand though, and simply worked around Natasha.

    Once they were done in medbay, Natasha carried Kate back to their apartment. Lucky was beyond ecstatic to see that his favorite human was okay but also seemed to realize she was hurt so he was very gentle.

    “Mama?” Kate asks once Natasha walked them into Kate’s room, “Can I take a shower?”

    “Of course, malen'kiy yastreb,” Natasha smiles softly, setting Kate on the bed and going to her dresser to pick out her softest pajamas. She helped Kate into the bathroom, and put her clothes on the counter, “Do you need or want any help?”

    “I don’t think so,” Kate says looking down, then she hesitates and looks back at Natasha, “When I get in the shower, can you stand watch outside?”

    “Of course, baby,” Natasha leans down and kisses Kate’s temple, “I will be right outside if you need me okay.”

    Kate nods, and Natasha walks out of the bathroom.

    As soon as she leaves, Kate feels her heartrate pick up. She takes a few deep breaths to calm herself down.

    She is just outside. You will see her after your shower. You are safe.

    She repeats this mantra in her head over and over as she takes the worlds quickest shower. Well, the quickest shower a person could take with a few broken ribs, a concussion, and a broken wrist.

    As soon as she was done, and got changed, noticing Natasha had brought her her ROMANOFF hoodie, Kate practically sprinted over to the bathroom door and flung it open.

    Her chest heaving and silent tears running down her cheeks, Natasha brings her into a hug immediately. After a few moments, Kate calms back down, and Wanda walks into the room, sending them both a comforting smile. She has changed and taken a shower, and looks to have clothes for Natasha in her hand.

    Natasha then slowly leans out of Kate’s embrace, “Baby, is it okay if I take a quick shower before bed? Wanda will stay with you the whole time. And I will take my shower in your bathroom so I won’t be too far.”

    Kate hesitates for a moment, before nodding. Natasha slowly moves out of the embrace, and grabs the clothes Wanda holds out for her. Wanda shifts herself onto Kate’s bed and pats the comforter next to her, “Come here, milaya.”

    Kate gets up onto the bed slowly, even though they gave her some pretty strong pain meds down in medbay, her body still hurts. Once she is sitting next to Wanda, she wastes no time in sinking into her embrace.

    “I missed you bug,” Wanda speaks into Kate’s hair, as Kate’s head is tucked under her chin, “We were so worried. But we knew that you were still fighting.”

    Kate pushes her head in further to Wanda’s neck, “I love you, Wanda.”

    “I love you, too milaya,” Wanda kisses her head. They sit in comfortable silence as they wait for Natasha to be done with her shower.

    When Natasha emerges from the bathroom, she smiles at the sight of her two favorite people cuddled up.

    They are here. They are safe.

    Breathing a sigh of relief, she notices that Kate can barely keep her eyes open. When she settles on the bed, Kate shifts over to snuggle up with her, and Natasha hands Wanda a brush, “Before you fall asleep, can Wanda quickly brush your hair, moya malen’kiy yastreb? I will re-brush and braid it for you in the morning,” she asks Kate.

    Too tired to verbally respond, Kate just nods.

    By the time Wanda is finished, Kate’s eyes have drooped shut. Natasha smiles at Wanda and nods for her to go shut off the light.

    As they are starting to shift to lay down, Kate startles awake, “Mama?”

    “Shh, it’s okay baby, we are just laying down to get more comfortable,” Natasha soothes.

    “Okay,” Kate nods, her eyes slipping back closed, “Love you, mama.”

    And Natasha thought she had cried out all her tears earlier. But she was wrong as a silent tear rolls down her cheek. She kisses Kate's temple.

    “Love you too, malyshka.”

    The next morning, Wanda awoke to find Kate still snuggled up on Natasha’s chest with Natasha still awake looking down at Kate.

    “Did you sleep at all, detka?” Wanda asked softly.

    Natasha shook her head no, “I didn’t; I stayed up just in case she needed me.”

    Wanda frowns ever so slightly, “I can stay up with her for a while if you want to get some rest?”

    “It’s okay,” Natasha shrugs slightly sending Wanda a small smile, “I don’t think I can right now even if I tried.”

    “If you are sure,” Wanda says slowly getting up, “I think I am gonna go make a pot of coffee real quick.”

    Natasha nodded and watched Wanda leave the room.

    I know I probably should sleep. I mean I haven’t slept since before Kate was taken, but I don’t think I can take my eyes off of her right now. What if something happens?

    As Natasha waited for Kate to wake up, she mentally prepared herself for many different possible reactions from the teen. Would she still be scared? Angry? Panicked? Who knows.

    But Natasha was ready for anything.

But then this one came into my life, and I will be forever grateful for her.”

    Most of the days that follow are similar. Wanda would wake up to find an increasingly tired Natasha already awake watching over Kate. Then they would all go into the living room to watch some TV, sometimes down to the Common Room; the first Family Dinner Night after Kate had been rescued, they had a small relaxing celebration with everyone. They would eat meals. Then they would go to bed.

    And almost every time she fell asleep, Kate would wake up from some sort of nightmare; Natasha and Wanda would be there to soothe her, reassure her, and help her back to sleep. As the days went on, Kate started to feel better during the daytime.

    Yeah, her body still hurt, closed doors were still upsetting to her for some reason, and she was still getting nightmares at night; but during the day, she was ready to be doing more than just watch TV and laying around.

    “Please, baby?” Natasha tries again, “I want to apologize.”

    There is no response for a few moments before Natasha hears some shuffling, then the door opens to reveal a teary-eyed Kate.

    “Oh malyshka, I am so sorry,” Natasha says sincerely, leading them back to sit on the bed. She takes Kate’s hands, “Everything that happened back there was my fault. I was so scared when I woke up and you weren’t here, I let my mind tell me that you had been taken again. I am not mad at you, I promise. I am so sorry baby,” tears start to roll down her cheeks.

    “You really scared me,” Kate sniffles softly looking down at their hands, “you have never yelled at me like that before.”

    “I know, and I am so sorry baby. I am not mad at you, I promise,” Natasha says sincerely, “I let my own fears get in the way. I was scared and wanted to keep you safe.”

    Kate looks up at Natasha to see that Natasha is indeed telling the truth.

    “You promise you aren't mad?”

    “I promise baby,” Natasha says wholeheartedly, feeling the tears slip down her cheeks at the fact she scared her own daughter, “I am not mad; I was just scared.”

    “Why didn’t you just tell me?” Kate asks softly.

    “I don’t know,” Natasha replies softly, looking down at their hands, “These past few days, I have forgotten that in order for me to be able to make sure you are okay, I need to make sure I am also okay. I just want to protect you. I have been neglecting things which caused me to lash out this morning… I am sorry malyshka… I guess a part of me feels responsible for what happened to you.”

    At this Kate puts her hand under Natasha’s chin and pulls it up slightly so that she can look at her.

    “That might just be the stupidest thing you have ever said,” Kate chuckles lightly before turning more serious, “The only people who are responsible for this are currently rotting away prison. Don’t you dare put yourself on that list. As for everything else, you just being here is all I need. I don’t need you to stay up all night keeping guard. All I need is my Mama.”

    Natasha pulls Kate into a tight hug.

    "I am sorry, Kate."

    "I forgive you, Mama."

    Natasha kisses Kate's temple, “What did I do to deserve you, malen’kiy yastreb?”

    “That’s a silly question,” Kate smiles, settling into Natasha’s embrace, “What should I start with? Saving countless innocent lives? Being dealt a shitty hand? Protecting Aunt Lena when you were younger? Taking down the Red Room? Saving my life? The Battle of New York?

    Joining SHIELD…”

    Kate continued to try and list every good thing Natasha has done, eventually getting Natasha to laugh as she added things like “introducing Steve to Twitter” and “telling Clint to ditch the mohawk.”

    Once they were done laughing, Natasha leaned out of the embrace slightly to look at Kate. “I love you, moya malen’kiy yastreb.”

    “I love you too, Mama.”
