    Meeting Babushka and Dedushka Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes A week or so later, when Kate’s injuries started to heal a bit more, Natasha announced that they would be going on a family trip to Russia to visit with Melina and Alexei.

    “Thank god,” Yelena sighs dramatically, “Now Mama can finally stop asking me when you are coming to visit.”

    Natasha and Kate chuckle.

    “There is only one slight hiccup. Well, actually two. As you know, Wanda just got called on a mission, and…” Natasha pauses.

    “And what, Natka?” Yelena asks, curiously.

    “And… I may have forgot to tell Mama and Alexei about Kate.”

    “Wait,” Kate interjects, “What?”

    As they pack up the Quinjet and get Lucky all ready for their ride, Natasha explains that she had been meaning to tell Melina and Alexei about Kate; but when they first reconnected, she didn’t trust them completely yet. And ever since then, she kept trying to think of ways to bring up the fact that she has a daughter without just coming right out and saying it. Kate completely understood, she knew how it was telling her friends that her mother was literally THE Black Widow; but it did make her slightly nervous to meet her mother’s parents.

    Are they nice? Will they like me? Will they be mad at Mama for not telling them sooner?

    Once they are on their way, Kate lets all her fears fall out of her head as she becomes enamored in watching their journey through the large windows of the Quinjet. She hadn’t been paying attention the last two times she had been on the Quinjet, and she wasn’t gonna waste it this time.

    They land on Melina and Alexei’s property in St. Petersburg around 4:00 p.m. local time.

    Grabbing their bags, the trio and Lucky make their way down to the house. Kate immediately feels her worries start to fill her head again with every step closer.

    Noticing, Natasha grabs Kate’s free hand and gives it a reassuring squeeze.

    “If you ever need a break, just let me or Lena know, okay?” She sends Kate a soft smile, “Alexei can be a lot sometimes, and Melina often asks questions without thinking about social consequence.”

    “Yeah,” Yelena adds, “You can even just text me if you don’t feel like announcing it, and I can take you somewhere for a break.”

    “Thank you,” Kate replies, sending them both a small smile.

    They walk onto the porch; Yelena steps up to the door and knocks. After a few moments, the door opens to reveal a very excited Melina.

    “Moi devochki!” She greets excitedly, immediately wrapping Yelena in a tight hug, “You are home!”

    Yelena lets out an annoyed groan, but returns the hug nonetheless, “Mama! I was home a few weeks ago!”

    “This is true, but I have still missed you moy svetlyachok,” Melina says releasing Yelena. Then she turns to give Natasha a hug, who hugs her back more happily.

    “Hi Mama,” Natasha greets softly, then pulls out of the hug.

    “Hello moy bol'shaya devochka,” Melina returns, just as softly. Then she notices Kate standing behind Natasha and is just about to ask about her when a booming voice suddenly fills the space.

    “AH! My family!” Alexei announces as he swiftly wraps Melina, Natasha, and Yelena into a tight hug, “together again!”

    “Ugh daddy!” Yelena whines, “Let us go! You are knocking all the wind out of my lungs!”

    “Never! It has been far too long—” Then he pauses, noticing Kate standing awkwardly on the porch as well, “Wait, who are you?”

    “I was just going to ask this, before you came bruting into the room,” Melina says poking Alexei in the chest.

    “Hey, is not my fault I wanted to see my girls,” Alexei holds up his hands in innocence.

    “You could’ve been a little more graceful,” Melina scolds.

    “What you can’t fault a man for—”

    “Will you guys save it for later?” Natasha interjects with a slight chuckle. Once she had everyone’s attention, she continues, “Okay. I am sorry I didn’t tell you guys sooner. But Mama, Alexei, this is Kate; my daughter.”

    “Hi,” Kate greets softly, and waves awkwardly.

    “Wait,” Melina looks to Natasha, “Your daughter? Why didn’t you tell us?”

    “Oh my god,” Alexei says stunned before smiling widely, “I am dedushka?!”

    Yelena chuckles.

    “Well—” Natasha starts.

    “She is not yours biologically, correct? You did graduate from Red Room, da?” Melina asks, and Natasha instantly becomes uncomfortable.

    “I adopted her around Christmas—”

    “Wait, so you had her when we did all the craziness earlier this year?” Alexei asks, confused, “Why did you not tell us sooner?”

    Natasha sighs and rubs her forehead.

    They continue to ask more and more questions, and Natasha flusters as she tries to answer them as fast as possible. Yelena looks down to see that Kate is getting overwhelmed at being the center of attention, so she softly nudges Kate and nods her head into the house.

    Yelena gives her a quick tour of the house and shows Kate where she can put her things.

    “Why don’t we take Lucky on a walk to the convenience store up the road? I have been craving some of my favorite Russian candy,” Yelena asks her.

    Kate instantly nods, grateful for the younger Widow noticing her discomfort.

    As they walk past the trio still talking on the porch she calls out, “Me and Kate are going on a walk, will be back in a little while.”

    “Don’t lose my daughter, Lena,” Natasha pauses her current conversation to yell to Yelena. “What kind of Aunt would I be if I let her out of my sight?” Yelena jokes as they head down the road.

    About 30 minutes later, Natasha finishes answering her parents’ questions and filling them in on how Kate became her daughter. They are all gathered in the kitchen as Melina starts to cook dinner while Natasha and Alexei share a drink.

    “So, you haven’t had any contact with the biological mother at all since the trial?” Melina asks.

    “I wish,” Natasha snorts, then her voice takes on a somber tone, “Actually, about two weeks ago, she escaped prison and kidnapped Kate with her business partner.”

    “What?!” Alexei snaps, almost breaking the shot glass he had been holding on the table.

    “Yup,” Natasha says sadly, “But don’t worry, each one of the boys as well as Wanda and I have made some trips out to talk with her, if you will.”

    “Good,” Melina responds as she stirs the soup she is making more aggressively than necessary.

    “So just keep that in mind when you are talking and interacting with Kate this week. She is already kinda wary of parental figures,” Natasha takes another shot of vodka, "And Alexei, she is still injured so please be gentle with her."

    “Of course, moy bol'shaya devochka,” Melina’s face turns soft, “I am excited to get to know her.”

    Just then, Yelena and Kate walk back into the house with Lucky and several shopping bags that seem to be filled to the brim.

    “What? Did you guys buy the whole store?” Natasha chuckles.

    “I had to get all of my favorite Russian and other non-American snacks for Kate to try!”

    Yelena defends herself.

    “Yeah,” Kate chimes in, “I wanted to stop after a few, but Aunt Lena kept insisting because I have never been here before.”

    “Makes perfect sense to me,” Alexei agrees.

    “But none now,” Melina says as she puts a cover over the pot, “no spoiling your dinner; which should be ready in approximately thirteen and a half minutes.”

    “So Kate,” Alexei starts, “Natashka says that you like to do archery like the birdman does, yes?”

    “You mean Hawkeye?” Kate chuckles, “Yes, I do. I don’t like to brag, but I have pretty good aim.”

    “Don’t sell yourself short,” Natasha adds, “She came in first in the National Archery Championships this year.”

    “Oh my!” Melina smiles, “That is quite the achievement! Natasha also says that you like Math and Science?”

    “Oh yes! They are my best subjects!” Kate smiles back.

    “She is insanely smart!” Yelena interjects, “Moy plemyannitsa is the smartest student there ever was!”

    Kate chuckles and blushes, “I don’t know about that.”

    They continue to talk as Melina and Alexei try to get to know Kate better. Once dinner is ready, they sit in the places they used to sit in all those years ago, with the addition of Kate sitting in the chair next to Natasha. They all feel happy and relaxed as their family is starting to heal back into what it once was.

    As they are eating, they continue to talk, updating each other on their lives. Melina talks about her work with Yelena on freeing the Widows. Alexei talks about how he is currently building more housing on the property for the freed Widows, as well as working with a non- profit in town to build shelter for the local homeless population. When conversation lands on Natasha, she is quick to brush it off.

    “Well, I basically just told you guys all my new news,” she says gesturing towards Kate.

    “You have not told us everything,” Melina replies, then smirks, “How is Wanda?”

    Before she can respond, Alexei adds in, “Yes how is Wanda? When are you going to propose to her?”

    Natasha chokes on the water she was drinking and Yelena and Kate immediately start to laugh.

    After dinner, they moved into the living room to watch some TV and talk some more. Around 10:30, Alexei and Melina had declared bedtime.

    “Kate, do you need some sheets for the couch? Or will you be staying in Natasha’s room?”

    Melina asks, as they are getting ready to head up to bed.

    “Can I stay with you in your room, Mama?” Kate looks up to Natasha to confirm.

    “Of course, malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha smiles.

    Melina smiles softly at their interaction, “Okay. There are snacks in the kitchen if you wake up hungry at any point, and extra blankets in the closet down the hall,” she says as she sends Kate a warm smile and leans down to give Natasha and Yelena hugs, “Goodnight, my darlings.”

    They all head up to bed. Kate instantly snuggles deep into Natasha’s embrace, and they both fall asleep almost instantly.

    A few hours later, Kate wakes up. Nothing indicating why she woke up. She didn’t have a nightmare, at least not one she can remember. There does not seem to be anything out of place in the room.

    Huh.

    Then it hits her.

    The damn time difference! My body is not used to sleeping at this time of day. Day? Night? Ugh!

    She starts to feel restless.

    Not wanting to wake Natasha up with her restlessness, she very slowly and gently removes herself out of Natasha’s arms, and quietly makes her way down to the living room.

    She sits on the couch and looks over through the window next to it at the stars. She is amazed to be able to see so many. Wow. Much like the Barton’s farm, this place is so relaxing and quiet at night.

    I wonder if Mama remembers what she said about getting a place like this?

    Kate feels her mind and body relax as she becomes a part of the peaceful surroundings.

    Her peace becomes temporarily interrupted when she hears soft footsteps and a collar jingling. She looks up to see Lucky walking into the living room and a slightly sleepy Natasha, who looks slightly relieved once seeing Kate, smiling softly at her.

    “Hey malyshka,” Natasha greets softly as she sits behind Kate and pulls her back into her arms, “What are you doing up?”

    Natasha had, in fact, woken up when Kate left the room. She was a little concerned that the teen had been upset because of a nightmare.

    “I honestly don’t know,” Kate lightly chuckles, instantly leaning back into Natasha's embrace, “Couldn’t sleep, I guess. Time difference.”

    “Ah,” Natasha nods, “I see. I am glad that is the reason, and not a nightmare.”

    “You and me both,” Kate replies, “Wait, I didn’t wake you up did I?”

    “No,” Natasha lies, smiling so Kate wouldn't catch on, “I couldn’t really sleep either; time difference.”

    They both chuckle.

    Then they lapse into a comfortable silence as Lucky sits in front of them begging for some middle-of-the-night pats. Once he is satisfied, Lucky curls up on the floor next to his humans and instantly falls back to sleep.

    “So, what have you been doing while you have been awake?” Natasha asks, softly.

    “Just looking at the stars,” Kate smiles and leans further into Natasha’s embrace, getting comfortable, “there are so many here. I was trying to pick out some constellations.”

    “I love looking for constellations,” Natasha says then points up to a cluster of stars, “You see there? That is Orion’s belt.”

    “Wow.”

    “And right there,” she points to another cluster, “that is Gemini.”

    The next hour or so is filled with Natasha softly pointing out different constellations and major stars. They slowly start to get tired and shift to be laying down more.

    Eventually, they fall back asleep on the couch; Kate basically laying on top of Natasha like she normally does, cuddling her head into Natasha’s chest.

    As the sunrises, Melina wakes up and makes her way down to the kitchen to start to cook breakfast for her family. She is feeling very content having both her girls, and now Kate, all under the same roof again. When she gets down the stairs, she pauses upon entering the living room.

    Seeing Kate and Natasha snuggled up, fast asleep on the couch, tugs on her heartstrings.

    Smiling, she quietly makes her way over and pulls the blanket off the back of the couch to drape over her daughter and granddaughter, pausing to lightly caress Natasha’s cheek.

    This lightly rouses Natasha, who opens her eyes sleepily, “Mama?”

    “Shh, malyshka. Go back to sleep,” Melina soothes quietly.

    Natasha hums then smiles before her eyes slip back close.

    Melina smiles again, and leans down to kiss both Natasha and Kate on the temple before heading into the kitchen to start breakfast.

    for reference: moi devochki = my girls moy svetlyachok = my firefly moy bol'shaya devochka = my big girl dedushka = grandfather moy plemyannitsa = my niece You Three Should Come with a Warning Label Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes After a few days, Kate had warmed up to Alexei and Melina. Alexei had even been trying to get her to call him ‘dedushka’, which Natasha had told him many times to let Kate do that when she is ready and to stop pushing her. Melina was more subtle in her grandparenting efforts.

    Despite having nightmares most every night since they got there, Kate was still having a great time. It was her first time out of the country, and honestly as long as she was with Natasha and Lucky, she was relaxed. She did find herself missing Wanda, but she told herself that they would be home in a few days to see her.

    Today, Kate was currently hanging out with Yelena and Alexei as Natasha went to go visit another Widow named Antonia with Melina. Yelena had suggested they show Kate the rest of the property and Melina’s other animals.

    “So here,” Yelena leads Kate over to a small pen, “These are Mama’s pigs. They used to have the mind control like I did, but we made Mama free them. They are still really well behaved though.”

    “Aww they are so cute!” Kate says, reaching her cast free arm through the fencing to pet a smaller brown and black piglet that is towards the edge of the pen. She squeals and giggles when he decides to lick her palm, “Do they have any names?”

    “No,” Alexei answers, “Melina has one named after me, but the rest are just numbered.”

    “Well, I hereby declare this little piggy’s name is Chris,” Kate announces.

    “Just Chris?” Yelena asks with a fond smirk.

    “Yeah. Well... Chris P. Bacon,” Kate smiles.

    Yelena and Alexei immediately start laughing. Hard.

    “Oh my goodness!” Alexei guffaws, wiping a tear from his eyes, “That is a good one molodoy.”

    “What else you got?” Yelena chuckles.

    They spend the next hour or so coming up with some great names for the rest of Melina’s pigs including Ham Solo, Calvin Swine, Sir Francis Bacon, and Peter Porker. After they are done, they start to walk to another building on the property. When they open the door for Kate, she sees that it is almost like another training facility and it has weapons lining each wall.

    “Wow,” Kate says walking into the building.

    “This is where we work out and I train other Widows on how to free our mind-controlled friends. It is also where we keep most of our weapons,” Yelena explains, “But normally, Daddy is not allowed in here because Mama is afraid he will end up breaking something.”

    “Is not fair,” Alexei pouts, “That only happened once! And the window already had a crack in it.”

    “Either way, I don’t think it would be a good idea to hang in here for a while,” Yelena says as she watches Kate walk over to a wall with a bunch of arrows on them.

    “Oh my gosh,” Kate exclaims looking at all the different arrows, “I thought you guys normally used your Widow bites and batons; why are there all these arrows?”

    “Antonia sometimes uses a bow,” Yelena replies walking over to Kate, “I have always wondered what each of these arrows do.”

    Alexei then looks at both of them with a mischievous smile, “I did hear Melina say something about a new weapons shipment tomorrow. If we try out a few, I'm sure Antonia won’t notice…”

    Kate and Yelena smile back.

    Twenty minutes later, they are standing in the middle of the field next to Melina’s house where Alexei had set up a few practice dummies to aim at.

    “Okay,” Yelena says reading the bow, “I am going to shoot since Kate still has a cast and is still healing. Daddy, if everything goes to shit, you are to pick Kate up and carry her to somewhere safe, da?”

    Alexei nods, “Da.”

    “Okay, now,” Yelena reaches down to get an arrow, “Any guesses as to what this one does, malen’kaya ptichka?”

    “I have no idea,” Kate says honestly, “Clint and I make all of ours homemade, outside of the ones we get from Stark.”

    “Welp, it is now or never,” Yelena notches the arrow and pulls back.

    Little did they know, Melina and Natasha had just arrived back at the house, and were walking out back right as Yelena released the arrow.

    The arrow hits the dummy and immediately explodes, causing the other two dummies next to it to catch on fire. Alexei had failed to mention, the dummy was made out of some sort of jelly that immediately launched all over the yard; also covering the trio in blue goo.

    “Eww!” Kate chuckles as she wipes some of the goo off of her face. Yelena starts to laugh. “Woah,” Alexei says before he starts to laugh, “That was AMAZING!! Do another! Do another!”

    Before Yelena can reach down to grab a new arrow, a voice behind her speaks up.

    “Seriously?” Melina says shaking her head, “We leave for three hours and we come back to this?”

    They turn around and look sheepishly at Natasha and Melina.

    “You three should come with a warning label,” Natasha chuckles.

    “What?” Alexei defends, “We were just having some fun.”

    “Just having fun?!” Melina responds, “My grass is covered in ballistic gelatin.”

    “I would like to offer that this was all Daddy’s idea,” Yelena confesses, “Me and Kate simply went along with it.”

    Kate nods in agreement, smiling and softly giggling.

    “What! Yelena, you would sell out your own father?” Alexei says shocked that Yelena didn’t defend him.

    “Seriously Alexei?” Melina groans, “you are as bad as the children! And you will be cleaning up my yard.”

    Alexei just hangs his head and admits defeat, “Yes, moya lyubov.”

    Natasha just laughs and holds her hand out to Kate, “Come on, malen’kiy yastreb, let’s get you cleaned up.”

    After a shower and a change of clothes, everyone sits in the living room, except for Alexei who is still cleaning up the mess outside. Natasha is currently braiding Kate’s hair and Kate is finding it hard to keep her eyes open.

    “So besides fooling around with Antonia’s arrows,” Melina gives Kate and Yelena a half-

    hearted pointed look, “What else did you three do while we were gone?”

    “Aunt Lena and Alexei took me on a tour of the rest of the property,” Kate says with a yawn, “And we met your pigs. They are so cute!”

    Natasha and Melina chuckle.

    “She named every single one,” Yelena smiles as she sits down on the loveseat.

    “Really?” Natasha responds as she finishes Kate’s second braid and pulls Kate into a hug, “Every one?”

    “Yup!” Kate says as her eyes start to slip close, “My favorite is Chris, though.”

    “Chris?” Melina asks.

    “Yeah, Chris P. Bacon,” Kate chuckles softly as she shifts to lay down more, keeping her head in Natasha’s lap, “He is the little black and brown piglet.”

    Melina and Natasha laugh.

    “You will have to tell me all the names you have come up with, milyy,” Melina smiles fondly at Kate.

    Kate just hums back as she is too tired to verbally respond. Natasha brushes some hair off her face and softly caresses the bridge of her nose until Kate is fully asleep a few minutes later. “How was your visit with Antonia?” Yelena asks Natasha softly.

    “Pretty good,” Natasha responds just as softly, as to not disturb Kate, “We talked a lot. I finally got the chance to really apologize to her. And to my shock, she actually forgave me.” “That is great Natka,” Yelena smiles, “Though, I will confess, I knew she was going to. She told me months ago.”

    Natasha picks up a pillow and tosses it at Yelena, “Jerk! You could’ve helped my mental health improve a while ago!”

    Yelena just chuckles and throws the pillow back at Natasha, “It wasn’t my story to tell.” “Girls,” Melina says in a no-nonsense tone, “Stop throwing my pillows before you break something. Also stop bickering so loud, moya vnuchka is sleeping!”

    That night, Antonia and another Widow named Sonya came for dinner. When Kate woke up from her nap, she heard extra voices in the kitchen and went to go investigate.

    “So does this mean Alexei is permanently banned from the weapon’s shed?” A woman chuckles.

    “I think so,” Yelena laughs.

    Natasha notices Kate walking in and waves her over to come sit next to her, “Hey malyshka! How was your nap?”

    “Pretty good,” Kate smiles softly with a small blush forming on her cheeks, slightly embarrassed.

    Did she need to announce to EVERYONE that I was napping?

    “Oh!” Natasha says, “I have some friends that I would like to introduce you to! This is Antonia and this is Sonya! They are both Widows.”

    “Cool!” Kate responds, shaking both their hands.

    “Guys, this my daughter, Kate,” Natasha beams.

    I will never get used to calling me that.

    “Nice to meet you, Kate,” Antonia smiles.

    “Likewise!” Sonya says, “I will admit, I heard a little about you from Yelena. She talks non- stop about her amazing niece all the time.”

    Kate really blushes this time and chuckles softly, “Really?”

    “How could I not?” Yelena smiles.

    The Widows and Kate talk for a while; the Widows talking to Kate about her archery skills and the events of Christmas, mostly about her taking down the Rockefeller Tree. Kate learns that Antonia and Sonya are Yelena’s primary partners when it comes to freeing Widows, and that they have freed over 50 in the few short months since the fall of the Red Room.

    Soon, Alexei and Melina appear and announce that it is time for dinner and they all sit down to eat. Once dinner is done, the other Widows leave and Kate volunteers to help Melina with the dishes. As they clean the dishes, they talk.

    “How are you liking Russia?” Melina asks.

    “I really like it,” Kate replies then she pauses to think before continuing, “It is so quiet and relaxing here. In New York, especially where I go to school and the area where I used to live, it is always so noisy. Luckily the Compound isn’t as bad. But here, you can see all the stars.” “I know,” Melina responds, “I too often find myself hypnotized by the quiet around here.” After a few moments of comfortable silence Melina speaks up again, “I am glad you and Natasha found each other.”

    Kate smiles, “Me too. She saved my life.”

    “I am glad she did,” Melina repeats then smiles, “because that brought you into our lives too. I can see how much you have brightened her life. You saved her life too.”

    Kate looks through the doorway to see Natasha, Yelena, and Alexei laughing about something.

    “Way back then, when Alexei told me it was time to leave Ohio,” Melina says, “I never thought we could ever all be this happy again, let alone with each other. Every time I see them, my heart feels like it is about to burst with happiness.”

    “I understand that feeling,” Kate smiles softly, “When my father died, I never thought I would find happiness again. But then Natasha found me.”

    Melina puts the dish that she was drying down and turns to look at Kate, “You are one of the strongest children I have ever met; and there are two pretty strong ones sitting in that room over there. I know you have had quite some hardship in your life, but I believe now that you are exactly where you are meant to be. Just like me, Alexei, Yelena, and Natasha. And I promise, we will make sure it stays that way. You are an important part of this family now.” Kate smiles up at Melina, before surging forward and wrapping her arms around Melina’s torso in a hug, “Thank you, babushka.”

    Melina smiles and wraps her arms around Kate, leaning down to press a kiss on her temple, “You are welcome, milyy.”

    As Natasha and Kate start to pack up the Quinjet, promising to visit again as soon as they can, Kate finds herself not wanting to leave.

    “And you will send me pictures of Chris P. Bacon as he grows?” Kate asks Melina as they are walking towards the jet.

    “Yes, moya milyy, I promise,” Melina nods.

    “Alright Dad,” Natasha starts as she gives Alexei a goodbye hug, “Behave for Mama. I don’t want to get any phone calls saying that you have blown up another building on the property.” “That happened one time!” Alexei protests. Kate giggles.

    “Are you sure you don’t wanna come back with us, Lena?” Natasha asks.

    “Nah,” Yelena responds, “I have a mission coming up next week with Sonya anyway. But I will be back soon.”

    Kate walks over and gives Yelena a hug, “Be careful Aunt Lena.”

    “I always am, malen’kaya ptichka,” Yelena returns the hug.

    Then Kate walks over to Alexei, “Stay out of trouble?” she says to him.

    He leans down and whispers conspiratorially, “But where is the fun in that?” then he laughs, “Goodbye molodoy.”

    Kate laughs and gives him a hug, “Goodbye dedushka.”

    Alexei starts to tear up a bit, and pulls Kate a little tighter, careful not to bother her injuries. Once Kate pulls out of the hug, she waves to the family, then walks up the Quinjet ramp.

    Natasha walks over to Melina and gives her a hug.

    “I am so proud of you, moya bol'shaya devochka,” Melina smiles, “Have a safe trip home.”

    “Thank you, Mama,” Natasha replies with a blinding smile, “I will let you guys know when we get there.”

    Then she pulls out of the hug and walks up the ramp. As they take off, Kate waves down at Yelena, Melina, and Alexei.

    I can’t wait to come back.

Of course Kate was excited for that reason too, but she had some other reasons to be excited.     What the hell? Why am I awake right now? Not even Mama wakes up this early.

    Kate felt fidgety. And it isn’t just because I am excited for the party. For some reason, something just felt off. She couldn’t exactly put a finger on it, but there was just a small unsettled feeling in the pit of her stomach.

    “Shh, malyshka,” Natasha soothes, “Just relax.”

Natasha following a shortly after.

Steve laughs so hard when he opens his card that Bucky leans over to make sure he is okay. The card is meant for a 4-year-old’s birthday as it has a big ‘4’ on the front, which Kate wrote a ‘10’ in front of to make it ‘104.’ He also seemed to enjoy his books that Kate got him, especially the ‘Texting for Dummies’ book.

    When Steve is done opening his gifts, America sadly tells Kate that she has to go.

    “I guess Christine’s family does a cookout every year too, and she really wants to introduce me,” America says blushing slightly.

    “I get it,” Kate smiles, “When my Mama first took me in, we had a New Years party that week, and I was introduced to more new people than I could count.”

    “I don’t mind it,” America admits, “I have been alone for so long. But it does get exhausting.”

    “Well,” Kate smirks slightly, “If you get too exhausted and need a break, feel free to text me. I will have my phone on me all day.”

    America smiles, “I will definitely be taking you up on that.”

    “America! It’s time to leave, kiddo!” Christine calls over.

    “Welp, that’s me,” America points awkwardly over to Christine, then she winks to Kate, “Talk to you later?”

    “Talk to you later,” Kate confirms with a soft smile as America walks away. Mj, Peter, and Ned then walk up on her side.

    “What was that?” Peter says with a knowing smile.

    Kate looks to see Mj and Ned smirking as well.

    “Nothing!” Kate replies quickly.

    Once it started to get dark, around 8:30 p.m., everyone moved inside to avoid the bugs; and for some, the sounds of the fireworks. Kate, Peter, Mj, and Ned were setting up the Nintendo Switch to play MarioKart when all of a sudden, the doors to the Common Room from outside burst open.

    Thor strides into the room with a boisterous laugh, “Happy Birthday Captain Rogers! I apologize for my late appearance as I was waiting for some others to be ready.”

    When he says this, Jane, Darcy, and Valkyrie walk in behind him. Kate gets up to go say ‘hello’ to them much like Natasha, and the other original Avengers six do, but when another taller person walks in Kate stops in her tracks.

    Her heart rate and breathing immediately pick up.

    Loki.

    What the hell is he doing here?

    And what the hell is he doing talking to Mama?!

    Kate breaks out of her thoughts and marches over to the group. She pulls Natasha back by her arm and places herself in between her and Loki.

    “Malen’kiy yastreb? What?” Natasha asks, a little shocked and confused at Kate’s actions. “Stay away from her!” Kate nearly growls at Loki.

    Everyone in the group turns to Kate.

    Loki’s eyes widen and he puts his hands up in defense, trying to send her a soft reassuring smile, “I am sorry child. I was just trying to say ‘hello’ to Natasha.”

    “Well, too bad!” Kate stands her ground.

    Natasha puts her hand on Kate’s shoulder. Wanda approaches, having heard the commotion.

    “Malyshka, its okay. This is Loki, he is a friend,” Natasha tries to soothe.

    This makes Kate whip around and look at Natasha, “A friend?!? Mama, he isn’t a friend!”

    “I can assure you, milaya,” Wanda tries, “Loki is a friend now.”

    “Yes, Lady Hawk,” Thor adds, smiling and putting his arm around Loki, “I can promise you he changed his ways.”

    “Him changing his ways doesn’t bring my dad back!” Kate shouts, tears filling her eyes, “Because of him, my daddy is dead!”

    Kate sees Loki’s face crumple, and everyone starts to look at her just like when the whistleblower broke the news about her abuse from her mother. Sad and concerned.

    Tears start to overwhelm her vision, and she quickly runs out of the Common Room.

    “It is okay, brother,” Thor tries to comfort Loki, “That was in the past.”

    “It is not okay,” Loki sighs, taking a seat, his face mournful, “but I made my bed, and I must lay in it.”

    Natasha and Wanda send Thor sad smiles as they go try and find Kate.

    They find her in the archery range, sitting where she normally would be standing if she was shooting, with her knees pulled up to her chest and her head tucked into the top while her arms are wrapped around her legs, crying.

    Their hearts breaking at the sight.

    Natasha and Wanda each sit on either side of Kate. Natasha slowly and gently wraps her arm around Kate’s shoulder.

    When she does, Kate jumps slightly before realizing who it is. Once she knows it is just Natasha, she immediately unfolds and curls herself up into Natasha's side, sniffling miserably. Wanda puts her arms around Kate as well, and slowly rubs circles onto her back. “Sorry,” Kate mumbles.

    “No, no dorogoy,” Natasha reaches down and wipes away a few of Kate’s tears, “You don’t have to apologize.”

    “When I saw him… I just thought…” Kate trails off, with a few stray tears still falling down her face, “He already took away one of my parents. I wasn’t gonna let him take any more.” “It is our fault, malyshka,” Natasha says softly, “We should’ve told you sooner that he had turned over a new leaf before we fought Thanos and that him and Thor reconnected.”

    “We didn’t know; however, that Thor would be bringing him today,” Wanda adds.

    “It’s okay,” Kate says softly looking down again.

    After a few more tears and moments of silence Kate speaks up again.

    “Do you trust him?” Kate asks looking up at Natasha then at Wanda.

    “Not fully. But more than I did when we first met him,” Natasha answers and Wanda agrees. “I don’t,” Kate says resolutely with some tears still in her eyes, “I hate him.”

    “And that’s okay, malyshka,” Natasha replies, “You have every right to. You don’t have to talk to him or see him again if you want. We can go back to the apartment for the night if you want.”

    Kate nods. Natasha and Wanda smile at her softly.

    “Okay bug,” Wanda kisses her temple, “I was getting tired anyway. I will go down and get Lucky then meet you guys at the apartment.”

    Natasha helps Kate up and they head to their apartment.

    After a long shower, Kate snuggles in between Natasha and Wanda on the couch, not really paying attention to what is on the TV.

    Natasha and Wanda share a concerned look as they notice that Kate seems to be too caught up in her own thoughts to notice her current surroundings. They try to ask her if she is excited to see Clint and his family tomorrow, but she just nods and continues to stare off. Frowning, Natasha tightens her hold on Kate and rubs Kate’s arm up and down, soothingly.

    30 minutes or so later, Natasha carries a sleeping Kate to bed, where Kate has a restless sleep filled with images of the Chitari, Loki, and her father.

    Sometimes all you need is family Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes That night, something woke Natasha up around 3:00 a.m. Sitting up, she looked around slightly confused as to what exactly woke her up. Neither her or Wanda had/were having a nightmare, and she couldn’t hear any other noises. But something in the pit of her stomach woke her up.

    Maybe I will do a sweep of the apartment just to be safe.

    Natasha gets out of bed, gently to not disturb Wanda, grabbed her gun from her bedside table drawer, and crept quietly out of her room.

    After a quick check around the apartment, finding everything in place and nothing out of the ordinary, Natasha pauses in the kitchen.

    Something still feels off.

    So, she decides to go and check on Kate.

    Once she gets to Kate’s door, she slowly opens it to not wake up Kate. However, the more it is opened, the more she realizes that Kate is already awake.

    Kate is sitting on her bed looking out the window, with her knees up to her chest and her arms around them, and silent tears running down her face.

    Natasha’s heart breaks.

    She softly makes her way over to Kate, “Malyshka?”

    Kate tears her eyes away from the window and looks up at her mother.

    “Did you have a nightmare?” Natasha asks softly.

    Kate just shakes her head ‘no’ and wipes a tear off her cheek. Natasha sits next to Kate and immediately pulls her into her arms.

    “What’s wrong, dorogoy?”

    Kate just shrugs and snuggles into Natasha’s embrace, slow tears continuing to fall down her face.

    Natasha frowns.

    She is usually so open with me, especially lately, I wonder what is making her so upset and closed off. She has been like this all night.

    “Do you want to talk about it?” Natasha tries.

    Kate just shakes her head ‘no’ and burrows her face into Natasha’s shoulder, effectively cutting off communication.

    Natasha frowns again.

    She really doesn’t want to talk. I bet seeing Loki stirred up some emotions she probably tried to push down a long time ago. But why are her walls back up?

    Natasha shifts them down so they are both laying in the bed, and she starts to rub soothing circles onto Kate’s back. Kate lets out a soft sob as more silent tears start to run down her face. Natasha reaches down to wipe them off.

    “Shh, baby,” She soothes, “It’s okay. We are safe. You are safe. I got you.”

    Kate lets out a shuddering breath, shifts her position so she can rest her head over Natasha’s heart, and she closes her eyes. Natasha feels her take a few deep breaths before her breathing starts to even out.

    Natasha looks down at her sleeping daughter who still has tear tracks on her cheeks and her heart twists painfully. She will open up when she is ready, but I wish I could make her feel better now.

    In the morning, Natasha was hoping that Kate would feel better and that her mood would improve.

    Unfortunately, she was wrong.

    When Kate woke up, she was still very quiet and drawn in on herself; and to say that Wanda and Natasha were worried was an understatement.

    Obviously, this wasn’t how Kate normally acted. Even after her kidnapping, Kate seemed more anxious and scared, but she would still tell Natasha and Wanda what was bothering her. But now, she wasn’t scared or anxious, she was depressed. And she wasn’t talking very much at all, let alone telling Natasha and Wanda what was wrong. Natasha was this close to calling Kate’s therapist for an emergency session, but Wanda talked her out of it as it might push Kate back more.

    Natasha was just hoping that the prospect of heading to the farm today would help Kate feel better for the time being.

    “Almost ready to leave, milaya?” Wanda asked Kate as they were putting Lucky’s leash on.

    “Yeah,” Kate replies softly.

    “I bet the kids are excited to see you,” Natasha adds, trying.

    Kate gives her a quick half smile and a shrug in return.

    “Okay, I think I am all ready,” Wanda says, “why don’t we head out?”

    They walk down to Natasha’s Subaru, and everyone piles in. Kate puts headphones in and turns to look out the window. Natasha and Wanda share a concerned look.

    “Ya volnuyus' za neye,” Natasha whispers to Wanda in Russian.

    “Ya znayu, detka,” Wanda replies, reaching over to hold Natasha’s hand and giving it a reassuring squeeze, “Nadeyus', ona pochuvstvuyet sebya luchshe, kogda my doberomsya do fermy.”

    Natasha squeezes her hand back, starts the car, and starts the journey to the Barton’s Farm.

    Kate had quickly fallen asleep once they had started on their drive. And she stayed asleep for most of it. However, most of the time her sleep was plagued with haunting images of the Battle of New York, her father, and the Chitari.

    As they crossed the border into Indiana, Kate found herself stuck in a dream of that faithful day but instead of being with her father and Eleanor, she was with Wanda and Natasha. And in this version, they both were unable to escape the collapsing penthouse.

    All she remembers is screaming their names.

    Suddenly, she is ripped out of her dream when she feels a hand on her shoulder, slowly and gently shaking her awake.

    “Kate?”

    “Kate, malyshka, wake up.”

    “You're safe, bug.” Wanda?

    “It’s just a dream, baby, wake up.” Mama?

    Kate jerked up, her heart racing. Blinking, she quickly took in her surroundings finding herself still in the backseat of Natasha’s Subaru, that is currently pulled off the side of the road, with Natasha and Wanda looking back from the front seat at her with looks of concern. She looks down at her hands as she slowly tries to regain her rapid breathing.

    “Are you okay, malyshka?” Natasha asks her.

    Kate looks back up at them.

    “Yeah, sorry,” Kate says softly, slightly out of breath and trembling, “it was just a bad dream.”

    “Do you want to talk about it?” Natasha asks in a loving, reassuring tone.

    “No. It's okay,” Kate responds, trying to control her shaking voice and hands, “I'm fine.” The trembling has not escaped Natasha and Wanda's notice. They send each other a quick look before looking back to Kate.

    “You were calling out for us,” Wanda informs her, pausing briefly before adding, “are you sure you are okay?”

    Kate’s eyes go wide, before she feels them start to tear up, “I am okay now…… now that I know you guys are okay.”

    Wanda reaches back and places a warm hand over Kate's trembling ones.

    There is a beat of silence before Kate speaks up again.

    “It was about that day,” Kate starts, looking down at her hands and starting to fidget with the rings on Wanda's fingers.

    Natasha and Wanda exchange a sad look, realizing what Kate was alluding to; before turning their attention back to her, letting Kate take the time she needs to sort her thoughts. Wanda briefly squeezes Kate's hands.

    “But instead of my dad and Eleanor; it was you guys in the penthouse with me,” Kate continues softly, looking briefly up at Natasha and Wanda. A single tear runs down her face as she looks back down at her hands, “...and neither of you made it.”

    Wanda and Natasha's hearts both break.

    Kate feels Natasha reach out and wipe the tear away.

    “Oh dorogoy,” Natasha soothes, wanting nothing more than to jump into the back seat and hold her daughter as tight as possible, “It was just a dream. We are all safe.”

    After a brief moment of silence, Kate speaks back up.

    “I know you guys know that something has been bothering me,” Kate says, still looking down at her hands, “I honestly don’t really know what exactly it is myself. I think most of it has to do with seeing Loki.”

    “I am sorry, malyshka,” Natasha responds, “how can we help?”

    “I don’t know,” Kate says softly with a twinge of sadness and confusion in her voice, “All of the feelings that I pushed down when my dad died suddenly resurfaced last night because I never properly dealt with them in the first place… I just feel kinda numb today.”

    “That’s okay, baby,” Natasha replies softly, reaching back to place her hand back on Kate’s cheek, “We are gonna be there with you every step of the way. And when you are ready or whenever you want to talk about it, we are here and will always listen.”

    Kate sends them both a small sad smile, then unbuckles and leans forward to bring them both into an awkward hug.

    “I love you guys,” Kate says.

    “We love you too, milaya,” Wanda replies as Natasha kisses Kate on the temple.

    After a few moments, and Kate moves back to sit in her seat, Natasha speaks up, “Are we ready to continue on our journey?”

    “I am ready,” Wanda answers, “Bug? Are you ready?”

    Kate hesitates for a moment before talking, looking down at her hands, “Um, yes. But before we get moving, can one of you, I mean only if you want to, maybe come sit back here with me?”

    Natasha and Wanda share a small smile, and Wanda undoes her seatbelt before quickly moving in the backseat to sit next to Kate.

    Kate wastes no time in cuddling up into Wanda’s side, taking a deep breath before closing her eyes.

    Wanda meets Natasha’s eyes in the rearview mirror with a warm look, then Natasha pulls back onto the road.

    Kate sleeps pretty much the rest of the way to the Barton’s farm. This time, however, there are no scary dreams or traumatic images behind her eyes. And she is grateful for the peaceful sleep.

    The little family pulls up to the Barton homestead around 5:00 p.m. Kate jostles awake when the car is put into park, blushing slightly when she sees Wanda looking down on her with a soft smile. They all get out of the car and are immediately greeted with a squealing Nate running up to them.

    “Auntie Nat! Auntie Wanda! Katie!” He screams excitedly, “You are finally here!”

    Natasha reaches down and scoops him up into her arms, making him giggle, “Hello Nathaniel!”

    “Where is everyone else?” Wanda asks him.

    Before he can answer, the rest of the Barton family walks up to greet them.

    “Hi everyone!” Clint says as he hugs Wanda.

    “Hi Kate!” Laura greets her as she gives her a gentle hug, that Kate can tell she is trying to hold back, “How are you doing sweetie?”

    “Pretty okay,” Kate replies, chuckling slightly, “Slowly healing.”

    “Any progress is good progress,” Laura says pulling out of the hug, then taking one hand and cupping Kate’s cheek, “I am just so glad that you are okay.”

    Kate sends her a small smile, before turning to greet Cooper and Lila.

    After everyone said their hello’s and helped Natasha, Wanda, and Kate bring their bags into the house, they all sat down to a very delicious dinner, courtesy of Laura. During dinner, Kate seemed to become more of her regular self. Laughing and smiling, and even joking around with Clint and the kids. It made Natasha and Wanda feel better. Sometimes all you need is to hang out with your family and laugh at silly things.

    After dinner, they all sat down to watch a movie. Kate sitting in between Natasha and Wanda of course. Kate slowly started to fall asleep on Natasha’s shoulder. Once the movie was over, Kate jerked back awake. Clint had carried Nate up to bed halfway through the movie. Lila and Cooper had just said their goodnights and headed up to bed as soon as the credits started to roll.

    “Okay dorogoy,” Natasha says looking down at a very drowsy Kate, “I think it is time for bed.”

    “But Mama,” Kate pouts slightly, “it is only 9:30.”

    Laura looks over and smiles at the interaction; clearly happy at the newer development of Kate calling Natasha, ‘Mama.’

    Natasha chuckles, “Yeah, but you didn’t sleep at all last night and practically slept through the entire movie.”

    Kate looked over to Wanda to get some back up, only to see a look that said that she agreed with Natasha.

    Kate huffs slightly then sighs, “Okay. Are you guys coming up too?”

    “We will be up in a bit, bug,” Wanda says kissing Kate on the temple.

    Kate hugs Wanda then Natasha before telling them all goodnight and walking up to Natasha’s room. She had told Lila that she might stay with her later in the week, but tonight she wanted to stay with Wanda and Natasha.

    As Kate is getting into bed, her phone buzzes.

    She reaches over and smiles when she sees the message notification that had popped up on her lock screen.

    America, 9:31: Hey! i totally forgot to text you yesterday! i ended up feeling so awkward at the other cookout i ended up not even feeling comfortable pulling my phone out ‘:D America, 9:31: I need to tell you all about it! Like Christine’s family is nice but OMG was it awk!

    Kate wastes no time in texting her back.

    Kate, 9:33: OMG!! Awkward how???

    Kate’s smile becomes even larger when America immediately responds.

    America, 9:33: IT WAS SO AWKWARD! They kept asking me questions on how we all met and stuff. Then there was this one cousin she had that knew some Spanish and tried to ONLY communicate with me that way XD

    Kate and America continued to text for a while. America telling Kate all about the weirdness of meeting her new adoptive mother’s family, while Kate told America all about their current trip to visit Clint and his family.

    Before she knew it, Kate felt a whole lot better than she did in the morning and in the car ride over.

Given the craziness that has been this year and the past few months, Kate completely forgot that her birthday was even approaching. Honestly, she didn’t even know until she woke up and saw the date when she opened her phone.

    Damn. I guess I am 15 now. Huh.

    In past years, after her father died, her birthday was never a big deal. Eleanor would make some sort of extravagant post on social media about how her only child was one year older, but that was about it.

    No cake. No candles. No presents.

    And honestly, Kate didn’t mind.

    She never liked to be the center of attention anyways. It was just another day of the year. Looking around, she notices that Natasha and Wanda have already woken up for the day, so Kate decides to head downstairs. Walking into the kitchen, she finds Wanda at the stove flipping french toast with Natasha leaning against the counter nursing a cup of coffee watching Wanda cook.

    Kate smiles at the domesticity and the serenity of the scene.

    Natasha then notices Kate, sets down her mug, and quickly rounds the kitchen island to give her a tight, warm hug.

    “Good morning, malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha says softly then kisses her temple, “Happy Birthday.”

    Natasha chuckles, “What kind of mother would I be if I didn’t know my own daughter’s birthday?”

    Natasha just chuckles again before pulling out of the embrace, and nodding towards the kitchen table where Wanda is currently setting up breakfast, “Why don’t you go have a seat and eat some breakfast before the others-and by others I do mean Clint-hog it all. Wanda made your favorite.”

    “You snooze, you lose, old man,” Kate chuckles.

    After the rest of the kids woke up, everyone asked Kate what she wanted to do for the day. She really, really wanted to sling arrows with Clint and Lila, but the idea was sadly struck down by Natasha as Kate was still healing.     Once they got down to the lake, Kate went to take off the oversized shirt that she had put on over her bathing suit when a thought occurred to her.

    “Mama?”

    “Yes, malyshka?” Natasha asks, walking over to her with a bottle of sunscreen.

    “Is my cast even water proof?” Kate says looking down at her arm.

    “It is, don’t worry,” Natasha chuckles, “Now lets get some sunscreen on you before you go swimming.”

    “All done,” Natasha says softly, “Are you okay, baby?”

    Natasha returns it, and gives her a quick kiss to the temple, “Okay, malen’kiy yastreb. Go have fun!”

    “Are you guys okay?” Natasha asks them slowly, raising one eyebrow.

    So they have clearly seen Kate's bruises.

    As they look back to the lake, they see Kate smiling wide as she swims with her friends/cousins, making all three of them smile.

So, Kate and Lila decide to lay out on some towels near the bank of the lake. While they are chatting, Kate’s phone buzzes with a text message.

    America, 2:17: Hey! I heard it was your birthday!! Why didn’t you tell me it was coming up??

    America, 2:17: Happy Birthday Kate! 😊 Hope you have an awesome day! As soon as you get back to the Compound, we have to hang! I may or may not have a present for you 😉

    Does she know I am gay? It’s not like I have been trying to hide it, I just never talk about it. I have never really told any of my friends about stuff like this because Eleanor was such a raging homophobe.

    Kate rolls her eyes good-naturedly and decides to take the plunge, “All right, I suppose I owe you anyways… Her name is America,” she starts to blush again.

    If you told me a year ago I would ever be this happy, I would've laughed in your face.

    After cake is passed out, and eaten, Kate was surprised, again, by presents being handed over to her. While trying to keep her happy tears in her eyes, and not rolling down her cheeks, Kate opened up a new pair of arm guards from Clint, a nice hoodie from Laura, a new journal from Lila, a new Nintendo Switch game from Cooper, and a drawing from Nate.     While they were setting up the movie, Natasha took Kate to go sit on the front porch for a moment.

    “Not that I don’t love spending time with you,” Kate smiles, chuckling lightly as she sits down next to Natasha on the porch swing, “But why are we out on the porch?”

    Natasha smiles softly, “I wanted to give you your birthday present.”

    “But I already got your presents,” Kate replies confused, “All those books? I have been wanted to read them for so long.”

    “That was from Wanda. This,” Natasha clarifies, as she pulls out a velvet jewelry box and hands it to Kate, “is from me.”

    Kate’s eyes widen slightly as she slowly takes the box from Natasha.

    When she opens the box, she finds a gold necklace that has a sideways gold hourglass Black Widow symbol that is connected to the chain on diagonal opposite ends. Her eyes start to water as she looks up to Natasha.

    “Clint gave me this necklace,” She points to the arrow necklace that she is wearing, “because he told me that I am the best shot he never took, and that I have saved his life so many times, so this was his way of repaying me,” Natasha herself starts to tear up, “And you get this one,” she points to the necklace she gave Kate, “because taking you in was the best decision I have ever made... I don't know where I would be without you... you have saved my life.”

    The tears in Kate’s eyes break free and start to roll down her cheeks. She throws her arms around Natasha.

    “You have saved my life too,” Kate says softly, pushing herself further into Natasha’s arms, “I love you, Mama.”

    “I love you too, baby,” Natasha kisses Kate’s temple, “Happy Birthday.”

    This is the best birthday I have ever had.

    Chapter End Notes :') awwwwwwwwwww DISCLAIMER: I know canonically Kate's birthday is March 21st but I wanted to write this and the timeline didn't line up lmao Next is some more fluff :) Someone is coming to hang out with Kate at the compound ;)

    Making Heart Eyes out of Stars Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes The day after they got back to the Compound, Kate found herself standing in the mirror, fixing her hair for what felt like the hundredth time.

    I think it looks better down with my natural waves.

    Or maybe should I wear it up like I normally do?

    Or MAYBE should I go ask Mama to braid it really fast?

    UGH!!

    Why am I so nervous??

    Of course, Kate knew why she was so nervous, but she would never admit it to herself.

    America was coming over to hang out today and staying until Christine and Stephen took her home after family dinner.

    Huffing a sigh and deciding to leave her hair down, Kate adjusts her necklace and walks out into the living room.

    Natasha smiles as she sees Kate approach the couch, still wearing the necklace she got her for her birthday. Kate had barely taken it off since.

    “Why, don't you look very nice today, malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha smirks, playing dumb, “I wonder if it has anything to do with a certain portal-jumper who is coming over to hang out before dinner?”

    Kate picks up a throw pillow and tosses it in Natasha’s direction. Natasha tries to dodge, but with Kate being a Hawkeye, the pillow still ends up hitting its intended target. Natasha laughs.

    “I still have no idea what you are talking about,” now it is Kate’s turn to play dumb.

    “Are you sure, dorogoy?” Natasha sends her a skeptical look and chuckles.

    Kate blushes and refuses to meet Natasha’s eyes, knowing that she doesn’t have an answer to that question.

    “Since when did you decide to butt into my love— I mean my friendships?” Kate asks, exasperatedly.

    “Firstly, I am your mother and I have a right to ‘butt in’ to your love life,” Natasha replies, catching Kate’s slip and not letting her get away with it, “And secondly, you started it when you decided to ‘butt’ yourself into my love life first.”

    Kate throws her head back and groans, flopping down onto the couch.

    Natasha starts to laugh again.

    Not wanting to be one-d up, Kate then smirks mischievously, “Speaking of, Alexei did mention something very interesting when we were in Russia…”

    Natasha then stops laughing and her eyes whip over to Kate’s.

    “When are you going to propose to Wanda?”

    Natasha’s mouth opens and then shuts, a light blush taking over her cheeks. Kate starts to laugh.

    It is always funny to see Mama like this because she is normally so composed.

    “I—uh,” Natasha stutters.

    “I mean you guys have been dating for like 7 months, that is like 3 years in the sapphic world,” Kate giggles.

    Natasha opens her mouth to respond but quickly shuts it as Wanda walks into the apartment having just finished taking Lucky on a walk.

    “Hey guys!” Wanda greets then looks over at Natasha with slight concern, “Bug? Why does Natasha look like she has seen a ghost?”

    Kate just starts to laugh harder.

    A few hours later, Christine drops America off at their apartment on her way to go work with Bruce on something at the lab.

    “Hi America!” Wanda welcomes her in and gives her a quick hug.

    “Hi Wanda!” America returns.

    Kate then walks over to greet her.

    “Hey!” Kate smiles.

    “Hi!” America smiles back.

    “So I was thinking,” Kate says, “I know you have been to the Compound a few times, and Dr. Strange gave you a quick tour the first time you came, but, do you want me to give you a better one?”

    America laughs. Kate smiles.

    “I mean Dr. Strange is a cool and smart guy and all,” Kate continues, “But I do live here. I know where all the fun places are.”

    “The fun places?” America smirks, then she chuckles, “Okay, I’m down!”

    “Come on then!”

    Kate grabs her by the arm and start to show her all her favorite spots. The library, the archery range (which she promises to show her again when she is fully healed and can actually shoot), the gym, the lab where her and Clint build new arrows, the roof, and her favorite place to sit outside which was right under a big white oak tree. This is where she ended her tour, and since it was a nice day, they decided to sit for a while.

    “That was much better than Stephen’s tour,” America says sitting down next to Kate.

    “Really?” Kate looks over to her.

    “Of course,” America smiles, “and it also helps that the person giving this tour was much more interesting to listen to and a lot more fun to be around… and way cuter too.”

    “Well,” Kate blushes, “I am glad you liked it.”

    “It is so beautiful out today,” America says looking around at the lovely summer day, “Not too hot, not too muggy. It’s perfect. And this is an awesome spot.” America looks up, admiring the tree.

    “Mm-hm,” Kate hums, leaning back on her one un-casted hand and shutting her eyes, feeling the warm sunlight on her face, "I love to sit under this tree. It is great to read, or draw, or even to do homework under."

    After a moment, America mirrors her. But when she goes to put her hands back to lean on, the fingers on her right-hand land on Kate’s.

    Kate’s eyes immediately pop open at the almost electric contact.

    America starts to blush, and goes to move her hand away, “Oop. I’m sorr—”

    The apology dies on her lips as Kate, surprising both America and herself, boldly shifts her left hand so that a few of their fingers link.

    Kate looks to America with a small smile and a light dusting of pink on her cheeks. America returns smile and they both return to enjoying the sunlight, neither of them daring to move their hands or break the contact.

    They hang outside, talking about everything and anything until it is time for dinner. Kate can’t help but think about that stupid High School Musical quote about kindergarten. When they get up to head inside, America shifts her hand so she is holding Kate’s hand more properly as they walk.

    Kate feels like her heart is about to beat out of her chest.

    She called me cute AND now she is holding my hand.

    OMG!

    Okay, fine, I admit it. I really like her.

    They continue to hold hands all the way down to the Common Room, only letting go when Lucky charges into them when they walk in, demanding pats.

    Natasha and Wanda share a knowing look when they see the two teens walk into the room.

    “Okay girls,” Natasha calls, “time for dinner.”

    Everyone gathers around the table, America sitting next to Kate, as they all dig into the amazing tacos courtesy of Bruce. They all catch up on each other’s past weeks, Natasha, Wanda, and Kate talk about their visit with the Bartons, Dr. Strange talks about his current new recruits at the Sanctum, Christine and Bruce talk about their project, and Bucky and Steve talk about their recent mission with Sam, who is currently out with his girlfriend. When dinner is finished, Kate watches as Christine passes America an envelope.

    “Aw sweet thanks!” America says, taking the envelope from Christine, “I completely forgot this earlier.”

    “No problem, honey,” Christine smiles.

    America then turns to Kate, “While everyone is cleaning dishes, let’s go hang out for a few minutes.”

    “Where?” Kate asks.

    “Follow me,” America smiles, and takes Kate’s hand, leading her out of the room.

    Christine chuckles and looks over to Wanda and Natasha.

    “Looks like we might be in-laws soon,” Christine jokes.

    Wanda and Natasha laugh.

    America takes Kate up to the roof. They both sit down taking in the view. It is a beautifully clear night. Some stars are even visible, and the glow of the city off in the distance reflects on America’s face as she turns to Kate.

    Wow. She is beautiful.

    “So,” America says looking down at the envelope in her hands, “I kinda forgot to bring this earlier… I guess I might have been a little nervous today,” she chuckles lightly and runs a hand through her hand and gives Kate the envelope, “anyways, Happy Belated Birthday!”

    Kate smiles, “You didn’t have to get me anything.”

    “Well too bad because I wanted to,” America smirks, “and if it makes you feel any better, I didn’t pay for it. So, open it, open it.”

    Kate chuckles lightly, and opens the envelope. Inside is two movie tickets. Kate can’t read them properly, so she takes them out and reads the name across the top. They were two tickets to the new Hunger Games prequel movie that was set to come out in November.

    “No way!” Kate smiles.

    America chuckles, “I heard that you were really into something called the Hunger Games, and I saw that a new movie was coming out. So I was wondering… if you maybe wanted to go with me? I mean I have never seen any of the others, but…”

    “Oh my god, yes!” Kate exclaims, then chuckles calming herself slightly, “Um I mean, yes I would love too. And maybe before, I could show you the other movies?”

    America smiles, “That sounds awesome. It’s a date.”

    Date?

    Date!

    Kate’s smile becomes bigger, “Amazing. It’s a date.”

    America smiles back and her eyes get soft.

    She leans closer and takes one of Kate’s hands. Kate sees America shift even closer and her eyes drop down to her lips before going back up to meet Kate’s eyes. Kate finds herself also shifting closer, as her tongue darts out to wet her lips. Her eyes start to close, and just as they are both about to close the gap—

    “Miss Chavez, Agent Strange and Dr. Palmer want to alert you that it is time to leave. They would like for you to return to the Common Room,” FRIDAY suddenly announces.

    The two teens immediately jump back, both of them blushing.

    Thanks a lot FRIDAY.

    Kate internally groans. She chuckles softly as America smiles and runs a hand through her hair.

    “I should, uh, probably head back down,” America says, smile not leaving her face.

    “Yeah that, um, makes sense,” Kate replies, also still smiling.

    America takes her hand, and they both head back down to the Common Room.

    When they walk into the Common Room, Natasha immediately notices a shift in the air between the two and smiles softly. Christine had told her how America asked her to get two tickets for the new Hunger Games movie for her, and that she had a sneaking suspicion what it was for. Meeting Christine’s eyeline, Natasha knows that she was correct, and sends her a smirk with one eyebrow raised.

    After Strange, Christine, and America had said goodbye to everyone, and they had turned to leave and started to walk out the door.

    “Do you have everything you need, kid?” Stephen asked America, seeing the thoughtful look she had on her face.

    “Wait!” America says, “There is one more thing.”

    America turns abruptly and heads over to Kate, who is currently standing with Wanda and Natasha getting ready to head back up back to their apartment, everyone else had already left the Common Room.

    She walks up to Kate. Kate turns around and smiles at America.

    “Hey,” Kate says, “did you forget something?”

    “Yeah,” America replies, “this.”

    Then she kisses Kate softly on the cheek, letting it last for a few moments, not rushing it. Kate gasps softly and instantly blushes.

    America pulls back and smiles softly at Kate, “See you soon.”

    “Mm-hm,” Kate just hums back, smiling so hard her cheeks will hurt. As soon as her brain reboots she replies, “see you soon.”

    America then turns around and jogs lightly to go catch back up with Strange and Christine. Kate brings a hand up and lightly touches the spot where America just kissed her.

    Natasha and Wanda smile softly.

    After a few moments, Wanda squeals lightly, not being able to contain her excitement, Kate jumps and turns around to face her and Natasha, who has started to chuckle lightly.

    “I knew you two would be a good match!” Wanda says delighted, “Now! When we get back to the apartment, you have to tell us everything that happened just now when you guys were gone.”

    Kate groans and rolls her eyes playfully.

    “Aw come on, malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha chuckles, putting her arm around Kate shoulders and starting to lead her back to the apartment, “remember what I said this morning about it being my right to know? And besides you kinda owe us after meddling in our relationship.” At this Kate looks up at Natasha offended, “Meddling?? If it wasn’t for me, there would be no relationship.”

    Natasha and Wanda laugh.

    “Oh whatever,” Natasha relents.

    Kate starts to laugh.

    “Seriously though,” Natasha says getting sincere, “we are very happy for you dorogoy. And we will always be here for you if you need us.”

    Kate puts one arm around Natasha and the other around Wanda.

    “I love you guys,” Kate smiles.

    “We love you too, malyshka,” Natasha replies, as she and Wanda each kiss opposite sides of her temple.

    Dog Days of Summer Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes Halfway through the summer, Kate woke up to the sun streaming through her windows.

    Sitting up slowly, as she would still get pain here and there, she smiles at Lucky at the end of the bed whose tail starts to wag as soon as he sees his human awake.

    “Come on, buddy,” Kate says to him, ruffling the fur on his head, “I think it is time to wake up, huh?”

    Lucky just tilts his head, but smiles up at Kate, then follows her out into the kitchen.

    Walking into the kitchen, Kate she is not surprised to see the rest of the apartment empty. She finally convinced Natasha, and herself, that she would be okay at the apartment alone if both Natasha and Wanda went back to work.

    It’s not like they are on a mission or something, they are still in the building.

    She is, however, surprised to find a plate of pancakes with a note next to it written in Wanda’s handwriting:

    ‘Good Morning, Bug! Mama and I had a meeting early this morning, and wanted to make sure you got some good breakfast :) We will be in meetings til about 4:30. If you need us, just ask FRIDAY to let us know!

    Love, Wanda <3 :)’

    Kate smiles then notices some writing on the back of the note. Flipping it over, she notices that it is Natasha’s handwriting:

    ‘DO NOT give ANY of the pancakes to the dog, Malen’kiy Yastreb! You need your nutrients!! And if you do, I WILL find out.

    Love, Mama p.s. seriously don’t give the dog any’

    Kate rolls her eyes.

    There is no way she would actually find out.

    She sits down, and digs into her pancakes. Once she has a few, she looks down to see Lucky looking up at her with the cutest face she has ever seen (let it be known that Kate has this thought most anytime Lucky looks up at her).

    “Aw! Oh my god,” Kate coos, “you are the squishiest, cutest dog there ever was!”

    Looking in between the plate and Lucky’s sweet face, Kate smiles and grabs a pancake, lowering it so the dog can enjoy breakfast too.

    “Shh,” Kate says, trying not to giggle, “don’t tell Mama I let you have some.”

    Lucky gently takes the pancake and puts it on the ground, before immediately starting to devour it, causing Kate’s giggles to break free.

    After they are both finished with their breakfast, Kate puts her dishes into the dishwasher then grabs Lucky’s leash to take him on a walk around the Compound.

    “Come on Pizza dog. Do you wanna go on a…” Kate pauses watching Lucky’s head tilt to the side, “walk?!?”

    Lucky instantly barks and starts to twirl in small circles, excitedly, as Kate struggles to get the leash onto his collar. Once she finally succeeds, they both head out the door, and through the many hallways of the Compound.

    While passing some windows on their walk, and seeing the beautiful weather outside, Kate decides to take Lucky outside to her favorite spot under the big white oak tree. She stops quickly inside the apartment to grab a blanket for them to sit on as well as the first Hunger Games book to re-read (America had told her that in preparation for their first movie night, she asked Christine to get her the series and she had stared to read the book, so Kate decided that she might as well read along too).

    Settling down on the blanket, Lucky lays down right next to her, and instantly cuddles into her side. Kate decides to set Lucky’s leash down on the blanket, not holding on to it.

    Lucky, despite being a very goofy dog, is very well behaved. He never pulls when they are on a walk, he always listens to his humans, he is very protective, he is so loving. And he has a tendency to stick with his humans and does pretty well 'off-leash.'

    He does, however, suffer from what Kate affectionally dubs ‘doggy-ADHD.’

    Things such as shadows, light reflections, and certain bugs can often get him so distracted that he gets tunnel vision and won’t listen until he can capture whatever has stolen his attention. The only thing that would break him out of that intense focus would be the promise of pizza or food.

    So, as Kate turns another page in her book, Lucky’s eyes immediately lock onto a butterfly that is currently floating near where they are sitting.

    In a sudden movement, he jerks his body up into a standing position to snap at it, making Kate jump.

    “Dude! What is that all about?”

    Lucky doesn’t even acknowledge her as his sole focus in life right now is this small fluttering bug moving around in the air just out of reach.

    Kate notices the butterfly and immediately tries to persuade the dog to ignore it, “Leave it, Lucky. It is just a little butterfly.”

    And just like before, Lucky pays no mind to Kate’s command and continues to chomp at the air, attempting to capture the winged creature.

    “No, Lucky!” Kate tries a little more sternly, “Leave the poor butterfly alone!!”

    Just then, the butterfly decides to fly out of the area around Lucky’s head and back over to the path near the Compound. And, of course, Lucky follows, running after it.

    Kate reaches down to grab the leash but it is too late.

    “Lucky! No!” Kate shouts, putting down her book, “Come back buddy!”

    Kate runs after him, chasing him and the butterfly around the side of the Compound. They keep running until they make it all the way back out to the front entrance of the operational side Compound, where the other agents work.

    The butterfly ends up landing on the tippy-top of a very intricate water fountain Tony had put in as a memorial for everyone they had lost throughout the years. Said fountain has a pool of water around it that many people throw coins in. Everyone walking around the entrance of the Compound pauses and whips their heads over to watch the unfolding event.

    Once Lucky reaches the fountain, his eye lock on to the top of the structure. He starts to bark at the bug and starts to circle the fountain. Realizing he can't get any closer, he pauses his pacing and sets his stance.

    Oh crap.

    Kate knows what exactly is about to happen.

    “Lucky, NO!”

    Sure enough, it is almost in slow motion, she watches as Lucky jumps right into the fountain and continues barking at the top of the feature.

    Kate tries a few more times to get him out of the fountain, before reluctantly stepping into the fountain herself to try and pull the still barking dog out by the collar.

    Lucky, however, is not fully satisfied that his human is fully protected from the butterfly and continues to bark and thrash around in the water, soaking both him and Kate.

    After a few trying minutes, Kate finally gets him out of the fountain. She looks like she just stepped out of the shower with her clothes on. Grumbling, she tugs on Lucky’s leash and pulls him back to the residential side of the Compound and brings him back into the apartment.

    “And to think I shared my pancakes with you this morning.”

    After taking a quick shower, and drying off the dog, Kate grabs her stuff that she left outside then sits in the living room to watch some TV.

    Ever since stepping foot back into the apartment, outside of drying him off, Kate has not paid any attention to Lucky, letting him know that she is not happy with him. And Lucky knows his human is not happy with how he acted.

    As she is sitting watching some TV, she feels a weight on her thigh.

    Looking down, Lucky had put his head up on her thigh, looking up at her with his one sad eye, almost as if to say ‘im sorry hooman. i love you.’

    Kate sends him a small smile as she starts to pat his head.

    “Aw, bud,” she bends down to kiss his forehead, “I can’t stay mad at you.”

    She pats the couch next to her to get him to jump up and cuddle with her, and he does.

    “But no more chasing things,” she scolds lightly, “and you have to listen to me when I call, okay?”

    Lucky licks the side of her face and Kate giggles.

    Kate hugs him and they snuggle up on the couch, continuing to watch TV.

    “I love you, Pizza Dog.”

    Wanda and Natasha walk into the apartment later to find Kate and Lucky adorably asleep and snuggled on the couch. Wanda moves over to them to cover Kate in a blanket as Natasha moves into the kitchen to start getting things ready for dinner.

    Natasha starts to chuckle.

    “What?” Wanda asks, smiling as she comes into the kitchen.

    “I told you she would give him some of her pancakes,” Natasha says, pointing down to the small amount of dried slobber on the floor where Lucky had his breakfast.

    Wanda laughs and shakes her head fondly, “Those two are quite the duo. I wonder what they got up to today."

    "Oh," Natasha chuckles, "I heard something very funny from Agent Anderson about them earlier. Something about the fountain."

    "Oh goodness!" Wanda continues to laugh, then smirks mischievously, "Should we see if FRIDAY has any footage?"

    "You read my mind."
