    Friends and More than Friends Chapter Notes slight SPOILERS for The Hunger Games (the first movie/book) lol See the end of the chapter for more notes Kate was practically buzzing with excitement in the passenger’s seat on Natasha’s car on the way to pick up Franny and Greer.

    She hadn’t seen them since the last day of school.

    And as we all know a lot of shit happened between now and then.

    Yes, she had been texting with them frequently, updating them on how she was feeling and such, but seeing them would be loads better.

    Maybe I will even tell them about America.

    Natasha looks over at Kate and smiles at the teen’s exuberance.

    “You excited to see your friends, malen’kiy yastreb?” Natasha asks.

    “I don’t think ‘excited’ is a big enough word for it,” Kate smiles.

    Natasha chuckles as she pulls into the driveway to Franny’s house. As soon as she puts the car into park, Kate unbuckles, texts Franny that she is there, and goes to wait outside of the car.

    A few minutes later, Franny exits the house, turns to see Kate waiting against the hood of Natasha’s car, and squeals as she runs up to Kate.

    “Oh my god, Kate!” She yells as she brings Kate into a tight hug, “I missed you so much!” “I missed you too!” Kate says returning the hug.

    “I know Peter told us you were okay and that you texted us but I was still so worried.”

    “I know,” Kate smiles sadly pulling back from the hug, “And I promise I will explain everything once we get Greer and get back to my place.”

    “I am just happy that you are here, now,” Franny smiles as she brings Kate back into the hug.

    Twenty minutes later, after a very similar meeting/pickup of Greer, they all walk into the apartment laughing like no time had passed. After greeting Lucky and Wanda, Kate turns to Natasha.

    “Okay, Mama,” Kate says, “I think we are gonna grab some stuff and go hang outside at my tree for a while.”

    “Sounds good, malyshka,” Natasha smiles, “I will come and get you guys when it is time for dinner.”

    “Okie dokie,” Kate replies, “sounds good! Come on guys!”

    The three teens head out to Kate’s spot under the big white oak tree. They all sit down and take in the beautiful weather. After a few moments, they start to catch up with each other on how their summers are going.

    “So,” Greer starts slowly, “I see that somethings have changed a bit since the last time we saw you.”

    “What?” Kate chuckles, pointing down at her arm, “You mean the cast?”

    “No,” Greer chuckles along, then turns more sincere and smiles, “well, not just that. I was more so talking about you calling Natasha ‘mama’.”

    Kate blushes slightly as she smiles as well, “Yeah. I have. I was calling her that in my head for a while before everything happened, and then once I was taken… I dunno… It just felt right.”

    “I am so happy for you,” Franny pulls Kate into a side hug, smiling along with the other two. “Me too!” Greer adds in, “And I am so glad you are okay. We were so freaked out when you didn’t come back that day.”

    Kate looks down at her hands, “I know. I am sorry guys.”

    “Don’t apologize, dude,” Greer says, putting her hand on Kate’s shoulder to get her to look at her, “it was not your fault.”

    “I know,” Kate replies, “but you guys didn’t ask to have to deal with my crazy life of supervillains and psycho biological mothers…”

    “Kate,” Franny starts, “we love you. We don’t care about all the crazy. Like I said earlier, we are just glad you are okay, and that is all that matters to us.”

    “Exactly,” Greer continues, “you can’t pick your homelife, but you can pick your friends. And believe me, if we didn’t want or couldn’t handle this, we would’ve stopped hanging out with you a loooong time ago,” she finishes with a chuckle.

    Kate chuckles and wraps an arm around both of them, “I love you guys.”

    “We love you too, Kate,” Franny replies.

    After a few moments, Kate then begins to tell them all about her kidnapping: how she woke up in a very sketchy room, how Eleanor had definitely gone off the rails, how Fisk used her as a personal punching bag (without going into a lot of details; she didn’t want to worry her friends), and how Natasha, Wanda and the rest of the team had saved her.

    “Wow,” Greer speaks up once Kate is done, “that sounds so scary Kate.”

    “For real,” Franny adds, “I definitely don’t think I would be able to last that long. 36 hours? That’s crazy. You are definitely a superhero, Kate.”

    “I dunno about that,” Kate chuckles slightly.

    “Seriously!” Greer says, agreeing with Franny, “Dude, you are so strong. What injuries did you end up with?”

    “Nothing major,” Kate replies, then begins to list them nonchalantly, “just a few broken ribs, some cuts that needed stitches, my broken arm…… and OH! My concussion. I keep forgetting about that one.”

    “That’s ‘nothing major’?!?” Franny asks wide-eyed.

    Kate chuckles softly, rubbing the back of her neck, “It’s not too bad. My concussion is pretty much fully healed, and I should be outta the cast by the end of the summer. The only real issue is my ribs, they take forever to heal.”

    Greer just shakes her head fondly, “What are we gonna do with you, dude?”

    Kate shrugs and cheekily smiles, “Do you guys wanna sign my cast?”

    Franny and Greer laugh.

    A few hours later, as they are all still sitting under the big white oak, Kate’s phone lights up with a notification. Kate looks down at her phone, not interrupting the story that Greer is currently telling about how her cat recently caught a bird in their backyard, and reads the message.

    America, 5:28: Hey Arrows! I am so excited to watch this movie tomorrow night. And don’t worry, I have finished the first book, so I am all ready to go 😊 America, 5:28: Can’t wait to see you tomorrow 🩷

    A heart emoji?!? OMG.

    Kate can feel her cheeks heat up as the small grin she had on her face turns into a full smile. Unbeknownst to Kate, Franny and Greer share a knowing look as they see Kate blush and smile. Then they chuckle lightly, breaking Kate out of her thoughts.

    “What’s going on there?” Franny smirks, “Got anything else to share, Kate?”

    “Yeah Kate,” Greer adds on, “who’s got you blushin’?”

    Kate chuckles, “it’s a long story.”

    “Well, we have nowhere else we need to be right now,” Greer replies, smirking and nudging Kate, prompting her to spill.

    “Fine,” Kate relents, “But I will only take questions at the end. Her name is America, and she is currently living with Dr. Strange and his girlfriend. I met her a few weeks ago, and we may or may not have a movie date tomorrow night.”

    “Oh my gosh Kate!” Franny squeals in excitement, “A date?!? Why didn’t you tell us sooner!?”

    “Seriously!” Greer agrees, “What does she look like? Is she hot? Wait—you said she lives with Dr. Strange; does she have powers?! Can she use magic?!”

    Kate goes to answer, but before she does, Wanda pops out of the nearest door and yells over to them, “Girls! Dinner is ready!”

    “Ah,” Kate sighs, then chuckles, “Saved by the bell.”

    Franny and Greer groan.

    The rest of the day is filled with more laughter and catching up. Kate told her friends about how they visited Natasha’s family in Russia and how they made the ballistic dummy explode. At the end of the night, after her friends had been taken home, Kate felt light. It was hard to believe that a little less than a month prior she had been at her lowest.

    The next day was also filled with more excitement.

    Today was the day.

    America was coming over to watch the first movie installment of The Hunger Games while their parents went out to eat.

    Not only do I get to hang out with her, we get to watch a movie from my favorite franchise. I hope she likes it. I know the first movie isn’t the best.

    …And maybe we will get to finish what we almost started on the roof the other night…

    She is shaken out of her thoughts when her mother knocks on her door, “The door is open,” she calls.

    “You look beautiful, malen’kiy yastreb!” Natasha beams, entering the room and sitting on Kate's bed, “Are you ready for your date tonight?”

    “It is not a date!” Kate grumbles, “Well its not-not a date, but it’s not an official date, so I don’t wanna hear anything!”

    Natasha then mimes zipping her mouth shut.

    “Thank you,” Kate sighs, then says under her breath, “I am already nervous enough as it is…”

    Just as Natasha is about to reply, Wanda calls through the door telling them that America, Strange, and Christine have arrived.

    “Okay,” Kate calls back, “We will be right out!”

    Taking a deep breath, Kate follows Natasha out into the living room to find Wanda, Christine, Stephen, and America all standing and talking near the front door. As she walks up to them, much like any other time she is in the multiversal-traveler’s presence, everything else fades away once she sees America.

    “Hey Arrows! Are you ready to watch this movie?? I am so excited!” America smiles.

    Omg. I love that she calls me that.

    Why is my heart beating so fast?

    “Yes!” Kate replies, smiling as well, “We got some good snacks to eat while we watch the movie. And I heard that you were a fellow pizza lover, so I had Wanda and my Mama order my favorite.”

    “Oh my god! That sounds amazing! And yes, I LOVE pizza. It is probably my favorite food,” America says.

    And pizza is her favorite too? Match made in heaven? I think so!

    Lucky bounds over, having heard the word pizza multiple times. Natasha and Kate both chuckle at the dog’s antics.

    “Well kids,” Stephen says, “I think it is time we head out to make our reservation.”

    “Yes,” Natasha agrees then looks to Kate, “If you need anything or have an emergency, call me or Wanda. And Kate, do NOT give any pizza to the dog,” sending Kate a semi-pointed look.

    “But Mama,” Kate replies playfully, “how will he live up to the name ‘Pizza-Dog’ if we don’t give him pizza?”

    She looks up at Natasha, and so does Lucky who sends Natasha a look that says ‘yeah hooman, I am pizza dog, why no pizza?’

    Natasha chuckles, then tries to be stern but fails, “no pizza. I mean it.”

    And with that, the adults leave, and Kate turns to America, “So… pizza?”

    America laughs.

    Once they are done eating pizza, in which Kate may or may not have slipped Lucky a slice, the girls set the movie up on the TV and sat next to each other on the couch.

    Shortly after Kate hit play on the remote, she notices America scooting slightly closer to her, before stopping and getting comfortable. Then after another ten minutes or so, she scoots closer again, making Kate’s heartrate pick up slightly. This continues until America’s right thigh is just ever so slightly brushing against Kate’s left.

    OMG. OMG. OMG. Keep it cool, Kate.

    Kate takes a deep breath, then tries to refocus her attention on the movie, which is somehow already at the point where Katniss teams up with Rue.

    Knowing what is coming, Kate braces herself to watch the one part of a movie that never fails to make her tear up. And just like she knew it would happen, as soon as Marvel throws the spear and it lands in Rue’s chest, Kate feels her breath hitch. America hears it, and turns to look at Kate, who’s eyes are still on the screen.

    Once Katniss is done singing and the scene changes, Kate feels some tears start to roll down her face.

    America pauses the movie.

    “Are you okay?” She asks Kate softly.

    “Oh yeah,” Kate softly laughs it off and moves her non-casted left hand to wipe at her tears, “This scene always gets me.”

    Before she can, America stops Kate’s left hand with her right, holding it. Then America's left hand reaches out slowly to gently wipe the tears off of Kate’s face.

    “I get it,” America replies softly, “When I first read this part in the book, I cried for like an hour.”

    America shifts her hand so that it is cupping Kate’s cheek, and she slowly and softly brushes her thumb over Kate’s cheekbone. Kate leans into the contact.

    Suddenly the air around them changes.

    It feels like how it did on the roof that night.

    America smiles softly, and Kate notices how her eyes dart down to her lips before returning back to make eye contact. Kate returns the smile and lets her quickly licks her lips, watching again how America’s focus seems to shift down for the brief action.

    Neither of them really knows who starts to lean in first, but soon they are less than an inch apart.

    Kate swears that she can feel sparks as soon as her lips touch America’s.

    Her eyes slide shut as America’s other hand reaches up to cradle the back of her head, deepening the kiss slightly. She lets her non-casted hand rest on America’s waist.

    After a few moments, they both pull back and Kate rests her forehead against America’s. She opens her eyes to see a goofy smile on America’s face; one that is no-doubted reflected on hers.

    “You have no idea how long I have wanted to do that,” America chuckles softly.

    Kate chuckles back, “I think I may know.”

    America chuckles again.

    “And I think, if its okay with you,” Kate musters up some courage, “I think I would like to do it again.”

    America smiles and meets Kate in the middle for another lingering kiss.

    America pulls back and looks at Kate, “I really like you, Kate.”

    Kate is pretty sure she can’t smile any bigger at this point.

    “I really like you too, America.”

    “Can our next movie night be a proper date?” America asks, hopefully, “Maybe we could get dinner or something first?”

    “I would love that,” Kate smiles.

    “Perfect!” America beams, “Now, shall we continue on with this movie night?”

    Kate nods and America presses play. America shifts and puts her arm around Kate’s shoulders, and Kate does not hesitate to lean into her embrace, slightly leaning her head onto America’s shoulder. Despite the dark and depressing theme of the movie, Kate finds herself unable to get rid of the smile on her face. And it seems the same can be said for America. When the movie is finished and the parents have still not returned, Kate suggests that they watch She-ra and America agrees.

    Might as well show her all my favorite things in media. I should probably introduce her to Reneé Rapp’s music next.

    Halfway through the second episode, Kate and America both fall peacefully asleep.

    The adults walk into the apartment to find Kate snuggled into America’s side with America’s arms wrapped protectively around Kate. After taking a few pictures and a collective ‘Aww’, Natasha reaches over to gently rouse Kate.

    Kate blinks her eyes open, and looks around; after noticing the position she is in, she can’t help the blush that rushes to her cheeks. Once she feels Kate start to move, America wakes up as well, also starting to blush.

    “Oh hey,” America says groggily, “you guys are back.”

    “Yeah,” Stephen smiles, “And I think it is time to head home, kiddo.”

    “Okay,” America replies. She and Kate both stand up.

    After America says goodnight to Wanda and Natasha, and while everyone else exchanges goodbyes, and Kate walks with America to the door.

    “I had a lot of fun tonight,” Kate smiles softly.

    “I did too,” America replies, smiling as well, “I can’t wait to do it again.”

    Stephen, Christine, Wanda, and Natasha meet up with them at the door. America leans in and places a lingering kiss on Kate’s cheek.

    “I will text you later, Arrows,” America smirks as she pulls back and starts to leave.

    “Talk to you to later, Merica,” Kate smiles goofily, watching America leave with a dreamy look on her face.

    Once the door shuts behind them, Kate turns to find a grinning Natasha and Wanda.

    “So wanna tell us why this ‘not-not a date’ has got you all blushing, malen’kiy yastreb?” Natasha smirks.

    Kate, who probably in any other circumstance would’ve denied anything and not said squat about what went down, just smiles, “Okaaaay, maybe it was a real date.”

    “I knew it!” Natasha cheers, “Now spill, malyshka. What happened?”

    “Yeah, Bug!” Wanda prompts, “I am at the edge of my seat here.”

    “Okay, okay, I will tell you guys,” Kate relents, leading them over to go sit on the couch before she began, “So, here is what happened…”

    Wanda takes a long look at Natasha before turning to find Kate doing the same. She gently and softly puts her hand on Kate’s shoulder, making Kate jump and break out of her thoughts. As soon as she turns to her, Wanda can see tears building up in Kate’s eyes.

    “I have to go get checked, bug, but I promise I will be back as soon as possible. Are you going to be okay?” Wanda asks softly.

    Kate sniffles and nods, not letting the tears escape just yet. She shakily responds, “Yeah. Auntie Lena is here.”

    “Okay milaya,” Wanda pulls her in for a tight hug and kisses her temple, then pulls her back to look into her eyes, “I will be right back.”

    “Okay,” Kate replies softly.

    Wanda then walks over to Natasha and quickly kisses her temple, whispering something neither Yelena nor Kate could hear. She sends Kate a small smile, then leaves the room.

    After a brief moment, Kate goes to sit in the chair near Natasha’s bedside. Yelena mirrors her, sitting in the chair on the opposite side of the bed.

    Kate scooches her chair closer to the bed then, almost as if not to hurt her, she very gently takes a hold of Natasha’s hand.

    “Kate?”

    She looks up to see Yelena looking back at her with a very worried expression. What happened? Did I do something wrong?

    “Are you okay?” Yelena asks.

    That is when Kate feels something drip down her face. She hadn’t even realized she had started crying.

    Kate just shakes her head no, before reaching her hand up to wipe at the tears falling down her face. She then settles herself into the chair, pulling her knees up to her chest and wraps her free arm around them, making herself comfortable; all the while still holding onto Natasha’s hand.

    By the time Wanda had returned to the room, it was well past 2:00 a.m. Walking in, she finds Yelena sitting on one side of Natasha’s bed, scrolling through her phone, and Kate on the other side of the bed, holding on to Natasha’s hand while she rests her head against the side of the hospital bed.

    Kate is barely awake and Wanda can see how exhausted the teen is.

    Yelena looks up and gives Wanda a small smile that she returns, and Wanda walks over to Kate, mentally gearing herself up for a conversation she knows probably won't go well. She bends down to be level with Kate and gently places her hand on Kate's back, slowly rubbing comforting circles.

    “Hey bug,” she says softly and she watches Kate’s eyes snap open wider and focus on her.

    “Hi Mom,” Kate responds, tiredly.

    Wanda takes a deep breath before continuing, knowing what Kate’s reaction will be, “Why don’t you go down to the apartment to get some sleep for a while?”

    Kate immediately sits up straighter, “No, I am okay.”

    “I know you are really nervous about Mama,” Wanda states gently, “but she is in the best hands right now, and she would want to make sure you are taking care of yourself. ”

    “No,” Kate replies firmly, “I am fine. I am staying.”

    “Milaya,” Wanda tries softly, “you need your rest. I can get you if anything changes-”

    “No Mom!” Kate snapping slightly, with tears starting to well in her eyes, “I am not leaving.” “Okay, okay,” Wanda says reassuringly and comforting, “I was only suggesting it. You don’t have to go anywhere.”

    Wanda moves to sit in a chair right next to Kate. Kate takes a deep breath of relief and slumps to put her head on Wanda’s shoulder. Wanda puts her arm around Kate and lets her lean into her embrace then kisses her temple. After a few moments, Kate speaks back up.

    “I am sorry I snapped,” she says softly looking down at her hands.

    Wanda softly takes Kate's chin in her hand and tilts her head up to meet her eyes.

    “It is okay moya milaya,” Wanda smiles softly, “There is no need to apologize. I completely understand why you don't want to leave.”

    Wanda kisses her temple again and Kate rests her head back onto Wanda's shoulder.

    “She will wake up,” Kate asks, “right Mom?”

    “She will wake up,” Wanda responds resolutely.

    She has to.

    After a few more hours of little-to-no sleep and the sun rises, Yelena leaves to go give Lucky his breakfast and take a shower. At around 9:30 a.m., Wanda starts getting paged for a debrief meeting with the rest of the team. She looks over at Kate, who is barely awake and who's dark circles seem to be even darker than the night before. She gently gets her attention. “Hey milaya,” Wanda says, “I have to go to this meeting. Hopefully it will not be long. I promise to be back as soon as possible. If you want, you can go take a shower and get changed, maybe even get some rest? I can have Dr. Cho alert us if anything happens.”

    And just like the previous night, Kate flat out refuses to leave. Wanda internally sighs, but chooses not to push her on this. She leaves giving both Natasha and Kate a kiss on the forehead.

    On her way to her meeting, she pulls out her phone to send a text to Yelena letting her know where she is going and if she could bring Kate some breakfast and a change of clothes before she goes to her own meetings for the day.

    Walking into the meeting room, Tony immediately feels the need to comment on Natasha’s absence, “Hey there Little Witch! Where is Natashlie? Aren’t you guys attached at the hip now?”

    Wanda rolls her eyes and takes a seat at the table. This is going to be a long fucking day, isn’t it?

    It turns out to, indeed, be a long fucking day for Wanda. The simple debrief meeting turns into an almost seven-hour long investigation on whether the car bomb that Natasha saved them all from was just from the arms dealer they were fighting or if it was a HYDRA sect. By the time she makes it back to Natasha’s room, it is well past dinner time. When she enters, she finds Kate in somewhat the same position she had been in when she had left. The only difference is that she is now resting her head on the bed next to her hand that is holding Natasha’s. Thankfully, it does seem that she has eaten some of the food Yelena had brought her for dinner and she has changed into some comfier clothes.

    Upon further inspection, Wanda finds that Kate is deeply asleep. She almost debates moving her into a more comfortable position, but doesn’t want to risk waking her up, as she is getting some much-needed rest.

    She decides to follow suit and gets comfortable in the chair she is sitting in. Just as she is about to close her eyes, she hears a faint groan.

    Her eyes instantly snap open to find Natasha slowly waking up. She gets up to lean slightly closer to her.

    Natasha blinks heavily a few times before her eyes settle on Wanda. She sends her a small smile before clearing her throat and letting out a raspy, “Hello, moya lyubov.”

    Tears spring to Wanda’s eyes as she chuckles softly, and replies, “Hi detka. You gave us quite the scare there.”

    Wanda leans over Kate to give Natasha a brief but meaningful kiss.

    “I know,” Natasha says, “I am sorry.”

    “Don’t be sorry, it is not your fault,” Wanda replies, sending her a soft smile.

    Natasha goes to move her hand, but finds it stuck. She looks down to see Kate asleep with her head resting on the edge of the bed, holding her hand tightly.

    “She hasn’t left your side since you got here,” Wanda supplies.

    Natasha feels something pull harshly at her heartstrings. When she tries to shift her hand again, Kate groans softly and tightens her grip around it. Natasha just chuckles lightly and moves her other hand to softly caress Kate’s cheek.

    “She looks so tired,” Natasha whispers. Wanda just lets out a hum of agreement.

    “How are you feeling, by the way,” Wanda asks, “I should probably go grab Helen and let Yelena know you are awake.”

    “I am okay, honestly,” Natasha lies but after getting a no-nonsense look from Wanda she adds, “maybe a little sore, and definitely tired.”

    As they are talking, Kate starts to shift. After a few moments, her eyes flutter open, and she sits up.

    “Hey malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha smiles.

    Kate’s eyes instantly fill with tears, “Mama? You are awake?! Are you okay?!”

    “I am okay malyshka, I promise,” Natasha reaches out and cups Kate’s both of cheeks lovingly. Kate can’t help the tears that fall down her cheeks. Natasha gently wipes them away.

    “You scared me,” Kate sniffles. Natasha's heart breaks.

    “I know, I am sorry baby,” Natasha replies, continuing to hold Kate’s cheeks in her hands. “It’s okay,” Kate says, “It’s okay now that you are awake.”

    Before Natasha can reply, Dr. Cho and Yelena burst into the room to check her over.

    After being told she needs to stay in medbay at least overnight (and rolling her eyes at the fact), Natasha tries to convince Wanda and Kate to go back to the apartment to get some actual rest. And of course, she is met with substantial protest that, if she is being honest, she was too tired to fight.

    She did, however, convince Kate to snuggle in the hospital bed with her. And she knew it was the right call when Kate’s eyes immediately slipped closed when she cuddled up to her side. Wanda watched on with a fond smile, and they both chatted softly throughout the rest of the night, content that they are finally home and safe.

    “Fine,” Natasha grumbles, “when can I start working out again?”

    “Probably in a week or so,” Dr. Cho answers, “But before you do, I am going to set up an appointment for you in a week and we can take it from there, okay?”

    “Okay,” Natasha pouts.

    “Nothing,” Kate replies, trying and failing to stop her chuckles.

    “What?” Natasha asks again, more confused and starting to get slightly annoyed.

    In a few hours, Natasha was allowed to go back to the apartment, and after giving a few pats to a very happy Lucky, she wastes no time in taking a shower. While she is in the shower, Kate takes one of her own, and Wanda makes a quick lunch for everyone.

    Soon, Natasha walks into the kitchen. Wanda smiles at her when she comes in.

    “Are you sure?”

    When Kate finishes her shower, she puts on some comfy clothes and brushes her hair before joining Natasha in the living room. She notices Natasha is trying to brush her hair, but that she is moving very stiffly. Without hesitating, Kate sits next to her and takes the brush out of her hand.

    Natasha turns to her with a slightly confused look on her face, but as Kate raises up on her knees and maneuvers behind her, the look shifts from confusion to a touched look. She turns back around, and Kate starts to gently brush her hair.

    “There is no need to thank me, Mama,” Kate snuggles into Natasha’s arms, “I am just glad you are okay.”

    “Four bowls?” Natasha asks, raising one eyebrow.

    Wanda just smirks and points to the door.

    “Perfect timing, Yelena, lunch is ready!” Wanda says.

    Natasha looks like she wants to protest, but after a concerned and pleading look from Kate, she just sighs and says, “Okay. I will. Plus, I think a movie day with moya malen’kiy yastreb will be way better anyways,” she finishes with a smile to Kate.

She knows from experience that if she asks Kate if she is tired or tells her it is okay to fall asleep, half of the time it works and the other half it doesn’t. So, she decides to shift down slightly so she is fully laying down on the couch and Kate snuggles right up with her. Natasha slowly starts to rub circles on Kate’s back. Kate lets out a content sigh and closes her eyes.

Have a great rest of your day, beautiful.’ Kate immediately blushes.

    Girlfriend?

    Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes The next day, Kate had to go back to school as the long weekend was over. She was feeling rather anxious about leaving her mother after the ordeal she went through this weekend. She had MAJOR surgery and I am just supposed to go back to school like everything is okay?? If I am not at home, who will be watching her to make sure she is okay and not doing anything she isn’t supposed to??

    Needless to say, Kate was hoping the day would pass as quick as possible.

    Unfortunately, that did not seem to happen. Every class felt like hours and nothing was helping time move faster. She spent most of the day anxiously bouncing her leg and chatting with her friends; and she was desperately trying not to keep sending Natasha texts asking how she was doing. After the sixth text in under two hours, Natasha just sent back a ‘I promise I am fine and will let you know if I don’t feel good; I love you malyshka, but pay attention to class.’

    By the time lunch had rolled around, Kate finally starts to somewhat relax as being with all of her friends slightly distracts her from what is going on at home.

    “So, then Peter asked me to be his ‘Guy in the Chair’ and I totally helped save the day from Lisa’s weird Dad who was stealing all the alien tech,” Ned says. Both Greer and Franny eyes widen slightly and they both let out quiet gasps of surprise.

    “Sure you did,” Mj responds, with a smirk. Kate chuckles softly.

    “I did!” Ned claims, then turns to Peter, “Peter tell your girlfriend I am not lying.”

    “Mj, stop antagonizing Ned, you already know this,” Peter tries to sound stern, but fails when he can’t control the corners of his lips turning up. Then he leans over to whisper, “let him have this,” in Mj’s ear.

    “Fine,” Mj says, rolling her eyes slightly, “I was just joking with you Ned.”

    “I knew it,” Ned responds softly.

    Kate, Franny, and Greer try to hide their chuckles behind their hands.

    Then Kate decides to put Ned out of his misery and save the conversation by asking, “Who was this ‘Lisa’s weird Dad’ anyways?”

    “He called himself the Vulture,” Peter supplies, “him and his gang were stealing a bunch of the Chitari tech leftover from the Battle of New York and were creating weapons out of them.”

    At the mention of the Battle of New York, Kate tenses slightly but quickly shakes it off. “That’s so crazy!” Greer adds.

    “What’s so crazy?”

    “Oh, Peter was just explaining your guys’ latest AP History assignment. Sounds like a lot of work,” Franny answers, helpfully covering their tracks.

    “Yeah that is gonna be one hell of a project,” Flash laughs slightly.

Kate sends her a soft smile before getting on the chair herself before reaching up to grab the bowl that Natasha wanted.

Kate shrugs, then turns her attention back to America, “My day was pretty weird actually. At school; you remember that kid Flash I told you about?”

    Natasha smiles, “They are! Kate told me yesterday that they have already had their first kiss!”

    “Really?! Aww, I am so happy for them,” Wanda smiles.

    “So?” Natasha asks.

    “So…what?” Kate responds.

    “So, what happened? What did you guys talk about?” Wanda pries.

    “Fine… ,” Kate just rolls her eyes half-heartedly, then she blushes with a small smile, “…she asked me to be her girlfriend. And I said yes.”

    Natasha smiles and wraps Kate in a hug while Wanda, honest to god, squeals in happiness.

    “Me too!” Wanda adds, joining the hug.

    “Thank you,” Kate says, returning the embrace, “I love you both.”

    “What?” Kate asks, confused as to why her mother is laughing.

    “You do realize that this means I HAVE to give her a ‘shovel’ talk,” Natasha says. Wanda starts to chuckle along.

    “Mama!” Kate cries.

    The Universal Cat Distribution System Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes By the time Thursday rolls around, Natasha is feeling a whole lot better, and due to her increased healing factor thanks to her altered serum, her stitches had already healed. This made Kate also feel a lot better; Natasha could tell that she was less concerned and anxious when she left for school in the morning.

    Now, Natasha is currently sitting in the living room watching some TV and looking over some old mission reports.

    Suddenly, FRIDAY announces, “Miss Chavez is at the door.”

    “Thanks FRIDAY,” Natasha says as she gets up and makes her way over to the door.

    “Hey America,” she greets when she opens the door, “Come on in!”

    “Hi Miss Romanoff! How are you doing today?” America asks as she walks in.

    “I am feeling a lot better, thank you for asking,” Natasha sends her a smile, “Why don’t you go sit in the living room and I will have FRIDAY get Kate. She is currently down in the gym.”

    “Oh,” America says, “I could just go down and grab her.”

    “Nah, its no problem. I will just have FRIDAY let her know you are here. It is time for Lucky’s walk anyways,” Natasha waves her off, then turns to speak to FRIDAY, “FRIDAY?

    “Sure thing, Agent Romanoff,” the AI responds. Natasha nods and goes to sit in the living room with America.

I still have many favors to cash in and many off-the-grid resources available to my disposal,” Natasha pauses, letting America sweat a bit, “Kate has had a very tough life, and it is my job to make sure she stays happy and healthy. She is also about three months old. Luckily for you, I actually still have some extra kitten food from the last kitten I fostered.”

    Next chapter will have some Kate and Nat comfort (with some Wanda too of c Kate and the Terrible, Horrible, No Good, Very Bad Day Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes For Kate, it has been just one of those days. One where nothing seems to go right and everything falls apart.

    It started out normal.

    Kate woke up with at her alarm and got ready for school. She met Wanda and Natasha in the kitchen for breakfast, then Natasha drove Kate to school.

    This is where it started going wrong.

    Walking into school, she usually meets up with Franny and Greer at their lockers. However, when she reaches their lockers, they are nowhere to be found. After a few minutes of waiting, she receives a text from Greer saying that she is not coming to school because she is sick. Then a few minutes later, she gets another text from Franny that she will be late because of a doctor’s appointment.

    Great.

    Kate starts to feel a low thrum of anxiety start to build as she no longer has the support of her friends and is alone.

    When homeroom ends, during which she texted Peter asking if he would come walk with her to her next class, she heads outside of the room hoping to see Peter waiting for her. But as she rounds the corner and exits, the hallway is empty.

    Then her phone buzzes with a text.

    Peter, 7:45: ‘Hey Katie! I am sorry Franny and Greer arent there, and I wish I could be there too but we have a Decathlon match today during school’

    Peter, 7:45: ‘Don’t worry tho, I will still be there at the end of the day to drive you home. You got this Katie! If you are feeling really stressed/anxious, text me’

    Kate texts back a reluctant ‘Okay, I will. Thank you,’ as she dejectedly walks down the hall to her first class.

    During her first class, which is World History, her teacher adds on to her torment by announcing a pop quiz.

    Kate groans internally and lays her head down on her desk.

    The teacher hands out the quiz an announces they will have twenty minutes to finish, and as Kate looks down at the quiz, she feels all of the knowledge she has learned about the French Revolution immediately blip out of her brain. Her anxiety from everything compounds with the stress of taking the quiz and cause all the words on the page to look like gibberish. When the teacher calls time and collects the quizzes, Kate knows she wrote something down; but she doesn’t know if it will even make any sense, let alone be correct.

    She spends the rest of the class anxiously picking at her fingernails and staring at the clock, willing it to go faster. Everything just seems so loud today. The lights seem brighter.

    Everyone seems to be talking louder.

    I can't wait to be home.

    Franny thankfully makes it back to school right before it is time for lunch. Which is perfect, now I don’t need to sit in the exact room where I got taken by myself. And Kate feels herself slightly relax. After lunch, they head to their next class together.

    Halfway through class, Kate feels a sudden cramp ripple through her stomach and settle at the small of her back.

    Oh no.

    No. No. Please. Not today.

    Then she shifts and tries to take a quick, inconspicuous look between her legs.

    And she sees a small red stain.

    DAMN IT!

    Suddenly she feels like crying. Pushing down the tightness in her throat she sighs and asks her teacher to go to the bathroom, and her and Franny leave the classroom.

    “Are you okay?” Franny asks, noticing the slightly upset look on Kate’s face.

    “No,” Kate whines, “I think I got my period.”

    “Oh,” Franny gives Kate a sympathetic look, “I’m sorry, dude.”

    “It just sucks!” Kate says, “It’s been so irregular this year because of everything that has happened, and I can already tell this one is gonna suck.”

    Franny gives Kate a side hug as they walk down the hall.

    Once in the bathroom, Kate realizes the stain is too noticeable, and will reluctantly need to change into her gym shorts.

    I need this day to be over.

    She can almost feel everyone’s stares when she walks back into the class room.

    Peter was right outside when the final bell rings, and Kate practically sprints to his car. She tells him all about her crazy day, and about how she can’t wait to go home and relax with Natasha and Wanda. When they get back to the Compound, she says goodbye to Peter and heads home. Walking into the apartment, she finds it empty.

    There is a note on the table from Natasha saying that she is at a meeting and will be home in a few hours.

    Suddenly, everything starts to get loud again and the lights seem too bright.

    Kate sighs and rubs her forehead, then decides to get changed into comfy clothes and go sit outside under her favorite tree. She sets up her blanket, takes off her shoes, and sits, just trying to take in the sounds of nature around her. After a few minutes, and some deep breaths, Kate feels the tension in her shoulders start to loosen as she relaxes.

    And again, it was just one of those days where the hits just keep coming; and suddenly, seemingly out of nowhere, it starts to downpour. Kate throws her head back and groans. By the time she gets the blanket, puts her shoes on, and gets inside, she already feels like she has taken a shower.

    Feeling utterly defeated, Kate makes her way back to the apartment where she takes off her soaked clothes, changes into dry shorts and pulls on a dry sports bra . She dries her wet hair, puts it into a bun, then climbs into bed, not even bothering to put a new shirt on.

    Lucky and Liho, noticing her distress, curl up next to her.

    Silent tears start to run down Kate’s face as she feels herself slowly slipping off into sleep.

    About an hour or so later, Natasha walks into a quiet apartment. She looks around, finding the living room empty. She is confused as Kate texted her that she is home, and I insured Kate was home by tracking her phone.

    She is about to call Kate when she hears a whimper coming from Kate’s room.

    She slowly opens the door and sees Kate asleep, curled into a tight ball on her bed with tears running down her cheeks. Frowning, she steps closer as she hears Kate cry softly again. Her heart breaks.

    Sitting on the edge of the bed, she gently shakes Kate’s shoulder to rouse her, “Malyshka? It is time to wake up.”

    Kate’s eyes slowly open, and sees her mother sitting in front of her. She sits up quickly and hugs Natasha, trying to snuggle as close as possible.

    “Are you okay, baby?” Natasha asks softly, “Did you have a bad dream?”

    Kate nods, and pushes her face into the crook of Natasha’s neck, still crying.

    “Do you want to talk about it?”

    Kate shakes her head ‘no.’

    “Okay, malen’kiy yastreb. That’s okay. You are safe,” Natasha soothes, rubbing her back, “We are safe.”

    After a few minutes and Kate’s tears slow, she pulls back. Natasha sends her a soft, sad smile as she wipes the tears off of her cheeks.

    “Sorry,” Kate says softly, looking down at her hands.

    “You know you don’t have apologize,” Natasha replies, putting her hand under Kate’s chin, making her meet her eyes. She sees that Kate upset, and it is more than just the nightmare that she just had, so she asks gently, “what is bothering you, dorogoy?”

    Kate starts to tear up again, “I just had a really shitty day. Franny and Greer weren’t there when I got to school, Peter was away for Decathlon all day, I had a stupid pop quiz in history, I got my period and stained my jeans and had to wear my gym shorts the rest of the day, then to top it all off, I got drenched in the rain when I went outside to relax when I got home. I am just so done with today, Mama.”

    “Oh, I am sorry baby,” Natasha says, “anything I can do to help?”

    Kate takes a few moments before she asks, “Can we just lay down for a while? Everything is just too much today.”

    “Of course, malyshka,” Natasha says, laying down and pulling Kate to rest in her arms. Kate takes a deep shuddering breath as she rests her head right on Natasha’s collarbone. Natasha starts to rub Kate’s back soothingly as her other hand gently runs through her hair, “I got you, baby. Just relax.”

    Once Kate falls back asleep, Natasha takes a second to send a quick text to Wanda to let her know that their girl has had a rough day. Wanda replies with a ‘I’m on it’ and Natasha smiles softly at the response then turns her attention back to Kate.

    An hour or so later, Wanda comes home with Kate’s favorite pizza, her favorite ice cream, a new weighted stuffed dinosaur, and her favorite candy.

    When Kate wakes up, she can barely contain her tears of love and gratefulness.

    They spend the rest of the night all cuddled up in Kate’s bed, Liho and Lucky included, binge-watching She-ra, and eating all the snacks Wanda bought for them.

    Before she falls back asleep, as she is currently leaning up on Natasha’s shoulder with Wanda’s arm wrapped around her shoulders, she can’t help but thank every star in the sky for bringing her and Mama and Mom together.

After a while, they pull off to the side and start to talk; catching each other up on their lives and gossiping about current events happening at the school.

    Kate then asks if anyone wants a drink, and goes into the kitchen with Greer following.

    “So,” Kate says as she grabs a red solo cup and starts to fill it with some soda, “What do you think about America?”

    “I think she is great!” Greer smiles, “She definitely passes the friend test. I am glad you two found each other.”

    “Me too,” Kate agrees, smiling, “And I can tell she likes you guys too. We will need to have a girl’s night soon!”

    “Count me in!” Greer cheers.

    As they are pouring sodas to bring back for Franny and America, someone approaches them, “Hey! I didn’t know you would be here!”

    Kate jumps and whips around to come face to face with Flash. Groaning internally, she plasters a small fake smile on her face, “Hey Flash.”

    “I like your costume!” Flash smirks, and Kate takes a moment to try and keep her eyes from rolling. And of COURSE he is dressed up as Spiderman. Smh. Greer does roll her eyes.

    “Thanks, I guess. Yours is cool too,” Kate replies politely, then grabs both her and America’s drink to try to send the social cue that she is done with her task and will therefore be done with this conversation, “well thanks for the party, I will see ya around.”

    Kate and Greer turn to leave the kitchen.

    “Wait!” Flash calls.

    Kate stops and sighs. Then sends Greer a pleading look. Greer rolls her eyes and huffs.

    Kate turns back around, “Yes?”

    “I was wondering,” Flash takes a step closer to her and smiles, “if you wanted to dance?” “I actually just finished dancing so—”

    “But you haven’t danced with me yet,” Flash says cockily, “Come on, Kate, I know you like me.”

    I think I actually just threw up in my mouth.

    “I am all set, Flash.” Kate says in a no-nonsense tone, “no thank you.”

    “Come on,” Flash pleads, “Just one dance?”

    Before Kate can respond, she feels a warm, protective hand slide around the lower part of her back and settle on her hip, “I am pretty sure she said ‘no thank you’,” America says in a firm tone.

    Kate instantly relaxes and leans into America’s side.

    Flash huffs out a chuckle, “Oh yeah? And who do you think you are?”

    “Her girlfriend,” America replies confidently, with a smirk.

    Flash’s eyes widen and his ego deflates in record time. Kate smiles sweetly up at America. Looking back at Flash’s defeated form, Kate is about to turn and finally leave the kitchen with America, but it seems America is not done with Flash just yet.

    At this point, there is a small crowd of people in the kitchen who are listening to the conversation.

    “I have heard a lot about you, Eugene. I have heard that you have been trying to hit on my girl for a while now,” America chuckles, “And pretty miserably, too. She has told me all about how you have been basically telling her how she feels about you. Why would you think she had any feelings for you, especially after you used to bully her all the time? And that's a whole another thing. If I hear that you start to bully her again or are continuing to hit on her; well… let’s just say it won’t be pretty.”

    Damn. I love her. I know she knows I can defend myself; but seeing her do it for me. Damn. Flash’s eyes are fearful and America gives him a sweetly sick smile. Kate leans up and kisses America’s cheek, then they turn to leave the kitchen. Just before they leave, America calls, “if you need any advice on how to actually attract women, let me know; I have tons of experience.”

    Walking out of the kitchen, they turn into the living room and pause before they reach the rest of their friends. Kate laughs, “Oh my god! Did you see his face?”

    America chuckles, “Well that’s what he get’s for trying to hit on my girl.”

    Kate looks at America with soft eyes, “Thank you.”

    “Anything for you, beautiful,” America smiles. Kate leans up and brings America into a sweet lingering kiss.

    As the party rages on, Kate and her friends are no longer annoyed by Flash and are basically high on life. Taking pictures, telling funny stories, eating a ton of candy, and dancing like there is no tomorrow.

    However. There is a tomorrow. And tomorrow, which starts at midnight, is curfew.

    Kate and Peter have semi-forgotten this information, and do not know exactly what time it is because both of their phones have died.

    After fourth or fifth time of dancing to the Monster Mash, Greer announces that her brother is there to pick up her and Franny. They hug everyone before heading out.

    “Wait,” Peter says, “What time is it? My phone is dead.”

    “It is…” Mj says pulling out her phone, “12:30.”

    Kate turns to Peter with wide eyes, “SHIT!”

    “What?” Ned asks, “What’s wrong?”

    “My parents wanted us to be back by 12! And we still need to take Mj and America home,”

    Kate says panicked.

    “Let me take Mj home,” Ned offers, “I was gonna head out when you guys left anyways.”

    “Thanks, man,” Peter gives Ned a quick hug and Mj a quick kiss, then turns to Kate and America, “Okay, let’s go.”

    They manage to drop America off at the Sanctum at 12:45 and pull up to the Compound by 1:15 a.m. Kate’s anxiety has been steadily growing since Mj told them it was already past curfew. They both get out of the car and start to walk down to Kate’s apartment.

    “They are gonna kill me,” Kate says nervously.

    “Yeah,” Peter sighs, “they are probably gonna be mad.”

    As they get to the door, Kate can tell someone is still awake as there is a light coming from underneath the door, but it is unnervingly quiet.

    Sighing, Kate slowly opens the door to reveal a steely looking Natasha, a slightly concerned Wanda, and a very grumpy Tony sitting in the living room. No one says anything as Kate and Peter walk further into the room.

    Tony stands up and walks over to Peter with a simple and firm, “Let’s go kid,” and Peter follows him dejectedly out of the apartment. Kate watches them leave, and as soon as the door closes, she turns back to Wanda and Natasha. Wanda has adopted the same steely look Natasha has.

    Kate gulps as she watches Natasha clench her jaw and tightens her crossed arms before taking a long deep breath.

    “Katherine?” Natasha says in an eerily neutral tone, with a slight amount of danger dripping in, raising her eyebrow, “What time did we agree that you needed to be home by?”

    Kate can’t help but feel like she is being interrogated.

    “Midnight,” Kate says softly, deflating slightly, looking down at her feet.

    “Yes, midnight,” Natasha continues in the same tone, “And what time is it now?”

    Kate hesitates. She feels terrible.

    “But Mama both me and Peter’s phone died and—” Kate starts to plead lightly.

    Natasha simply holds up a hand, “What time is it now?” she repeats, a little firmer.

    Kate deflates completely, and cowers under Natasha’s stern gaze, “After 1:00.”

    “Exactly. Over a full hour after that specified time.”

    Kate feels her anxiety skyrocket. In years past, if she was out past curfew (well if she was out past curfew in a moment of time where Eleanor decided she cared), she would get reamed out for being a disgrace and for being potential bad publicity for the Bishop name.

    “I’m sorry—”

    “Do you know how worried we were?” Natasha cuts in, with a slight amount of hurt and worry in her voice. Wanda puts her hand on Natasha’s shoulder and tears start spring to Natasha’s eyes, “We were so scared that something happened to you. It wasn’t until Strange let us know that you guys dropped off America that we knew where you were.”

    This strikes Kate hard. Tears immediately start to pool in her eyes. I expected them to be mad at me, not scared for me. She takes a shuddering breath before the tears start to run down her cheeks.

    “I am sorry,” Kate says sincerely, “I didn’t mean to worry you.”

    Kate wraps her arms around herself as she starts to cry a little harder. Natasha gets up from the couch and pulls Kate into a very tight hug. Wanda joins in shortly after.

    After a few minutes, Natasha pulls back slightly and wipes the tears off Kate’s face.

    “We forgive you, malyshka,” Natasha says, “we are glad that you are safe. Next time, you are to fully charge your phone before you leave, and maybe we get you an emergency portable charger.”

    “And you come back before curfew,” Wanda adds.

    Kate nods, “I promise.”

    “And because you came back after curfew this time, you are grounded for one week. No friends over and No America over, okay?” Natasha says.

    Kate sighs, but nods, “Okay.”

    “Alright,” Natasha hugs Kate again, “I am sorry for being stern, dorogoy, but I am only doing it because I love you.”

    “I know, Mama,” Kate leans into the embrace. Natasha kisses one side of Kate’s temple while Wanda kisses the other.

    “We love you, baby,” Natasha says.

    “I love you guys too,” Kate hugs them tighter, feeling a weight lift off her chest.

    Grounded Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes When Kate woke up the next morning, she felt restless. She had barely slept, tossing and turning throughout the night. She knew that Natasha and Wanda had both said they loved her and that they were no longer mad, just glad that she was safe, but Kate was nervous that they were just saying that. That they were actually still upset with her, and were just waiting til the morning to yell at her. She was waiting for the other shoe to drop.

    Despite it being rather early (Natasha and Wanda weren’t even awake yet), she didn’t linger in bed; as soon as her eyes opened, she got up and got to work. She immediately made her bed and straightened up her room, with Lucky and Liho both looking up at her like she is crazy for being awake this early. Once she was done with that, she headed out into the kitchen.

    When she got into the kitchen, she looks up to see that it is only 6:05. Sighing at the early time, she pulls out the ingredients to make homemade pancakes and bacon.

    Maybe I will make them some breakfast. Eleanor would always be in a better mood after I cooked for her. But they aren’t Eleanor. They wouldn’t yell like she did… right?

    The sounds in the kitchen slowly wakes Natasha up. Looking around the room, she tries to discern the source of the noise. Finding nothing, she nudges Wanda.

    “Wanda?”

    Wanda just hums and nudges her head deeper into Natasha’s neck.

    “Do you hear that?” Natasha whispers.

    “Hear what, detka?” Wanda yawns.

    “Someone is in the kitchen.”

    At that, Wanda raises her head and listens. After a moment she says, “it sounds like cooking. Do you think Kate is up?”

    Natasha shrugs, but gets out of bed anyway to go investigate, with Wanda close behind.

    Walking into the kitchen, they find Kate standing with her back to them at the stove, flipping pancakes. They both notice the slight shake in her movements, as if the teen is on edge about something.

    “Malyshka?” Natasha says as calmly as possible.

    Kate jumps and whips around to face them, her eyes wide.

    “Woah, easy milaya, it’s just us,” Wanda says soothingly.

    “Sorry,” Kate rubs the back of her neck, “I didn’t hear you guys come in.”

    “What are you doing up so early, malen’kiy yastreb?” Natasha asks, “Did you have a nightmare?”

    “Oh, no, I am good,” Kate replies a little too quickly, “I just woke up early and decided to make some breakfast, I guess,” she shrugs.

    Natasha and Wanda immediately pick up on Kate’s nervousness and jitteriness.

    “You just woke up early, and decided to make breakfast?” Wanda asks with a slightly skeptic tilt to her tone.

    “Mm-hm,” Kate nods, not making direct eye contact with either of her parents before quickly turning around to flip more pancakes, effectively shutting off the conversation.

    Natasha and Wanda look at each other, confused at their daughter’s reaction. They are definitely concerned about her odd behavior. Sitting down on the stools at the kitchen island, they continue to watch Kate frantically cook the pancakes and bacon.

    Once Kate is done, she divides the food up on two plates for her mothers and hands them over to them, “Here you go. Sorry it took so long; I haven’t made bacon in a while,” she says as she immediately goes to start cleaning the dishes and pans that she used.

    Natasha and Wanda, again, both share a quick look before Wanda speaks up, not really knowing how to respond, “Oh... um, it’s okay, milaya.”

    “Yeah dorogoy,” Natasha adds, “you didn’t need to cook us breakfast.”

    Kate shrugs, “I was up anyways.”

    After Kate finishes the dishes, she wipes her hands and goes to leave the kitchen, but she is stopped by Natasha’s voice, “Kate, wait.”

    Kate instantly freezes. This is it. Now they are gonna tell me off.

    She slowly turns around, “Yes, Mama?”

    “Aren’t you going to eat too?” Natasha asks, lovingly and slightly concerned.

    “Oh! I, um, didn’t realized I was allowed to…” Kate replies softly, looking down at her hands.

    Natasha and Wanda both look concerned.

    “What do you mean, malyshka?” Natasha asks.

    “Well, I am punished. And every other time I have been grounded previously,” the ‘by Eleanor’ is implied but not said, “she told me I wasn’t allowed to eat,” Kate says, still looking down at her hands.

    Natasha and Wanda’s hearts break.

    “Oh milaya,” Wanda says sadly.

    “Kate, I promise, no matter what happens, you will always be allowed food. You never, EVER, need to ask to eat. It will never be taken away as punishment,” Natasha says sincerely, as gets up and walks over to Kate, wrapping her in a tight hug.

    A single tear runs down Kate’s face, and Natasha wipes it off. Kate leans into the hug for a brief moment before pulling away slowly. Kate sends Natasha a small smile. Natasha returns it and places a soft kiss on her temple.

    “Why don’t you go sit down, and we will get you something to eat, hm?” Natasha suggests.

    Kate nods and goes to sit in the seat Natasha was previously sitting in as Natasha walks over to start on some breakfast for her. Wanda reaches over and pulls her into a tight hug.

    Kate takes a deep breath and tries her hardest to push down all the anxiety still swirling in her veins.

    After breakfast, Kate took Lucky for a long walk. The pup was a little reluctant at first due to the early hour, but shortly took it in stride and became more enthusiastic.

    By the time she got back, Natasha and Wanda were in full Saturday morning chore mode.

    Most Saturday mornings were reserved for cleaning the kitchen, folding laundry, and once Kate was awake, vacuuming the whole apartment. Wanda was currently cleaning the kitchen and Natasha was getting started on the laundry.

    As soon as Kate unclips Lucky’s leash, Natasha is expecting her to probably head back to bed since she woke up so early. However, Kate immediately moves into the kitchen, and Natasha hears her tell Wanda that she will help her clean the stove. Natasha watches as Wanda tries to tell her its okay and that she doesn’t need to help, but Kate just starts cleaning anyways. Wanda looks up to make eye contact with Natasha, and Natasha just shrugs.

    Once the kitchen is mostly clean, Kate, despite looking exhausted, dusts the living room before grabbing the vacuum and starts to vacuum the apartment while Natasha finishes folding the laundry and Wanda finishes cleaning the kitchen. Natasha and Wanda notice that the slight shake has returned to her movements.

    Once again, Natasha and Wanda share a look.

    Natasha quickly finishes folding the laundry, and goes into the kitchen to talk to Wanda. “YA bespokoyus' za neye. Kazhetsya, ona segodnya ochen' vstrevozhena,” Natasha says quietly to Wanda.

    “YA tozhe. Ona kazhetsya ochen' nervnoy. Dumayesh', eto potomu, chto u neye vchera vecherom byli problemy s nami?” Wanda responds.

    “Mozhet byt'. Eto pervyy raz, kogda u neye s nami problemy. Imeyet smysl, chto ona nemnogo nervnichayet; YA imeyu v vidu, chto my oba znayem, kak ona byla distsiplinirovannoy,” Natasha continues softly.

    “Nam prosto nuzhno zaverit' yeye, chto ona v bezopasnosti i lyubima,” Wanda replies.

    “Da,” Natasha says as they both look over at Kate who is still frantically vacuuming.

    The poor teen looks exhausted and restless. Natasha knows that she needs to fix this and fix it soon before Kate burns herself out.

    Once Kate is finished vacuuming, she empties the dust and dirt into the trash can before putting the vacuum back into the hallway closet. When she returns to the living room, she finds Natasha and Wanda sitting on the couch.

    Natasha then notices the bags under her eyes. I wonder if she got any sleep last night. She looks so anxious.

    “Thank you, malen’kiy yastreb,” Natasha smiles, “but, as much as we appreciate it, you know you didn’t need to help us clean, right?”

    Kate shrugs and looks down at her hands.

    “Kate,” Natasha says softly but firmly, taking Kate’s hands in hers and waiting until Kate meets her eye before continuing, “we promise we are not mad or upset at you because of last night. We know it was a genuine mistake. And we are just happy that you are safe. And we promise your only punishment is being grounded. There is nothing else coming. No yelling. No hitting. No withholding food… I promise baby.”

    Tears slowly start to fall down Kate’s face, and Natasha tugs lightly on Kate’s hands to bring her into a warm, loving embrace, that Wanda quickly joins. They both whisper soothing and comforting reassurances until Kate’s tears slow.

    Once Kate pulls slightly back, Natasha reaches and immediately wipes the tears off her face as Wanda kisses her temple.

    “I’m sorry,” Kate says softly.

    “Oh bug, there is nothing to apologize for,” Wanda soothes, “we know that this kind of thing is harder because of your past, but that is not your fault.”

    “I love you guys,” Kate says as she wraps her arms back around both of her parents.

    “We love you too, malyshka,” Natasha kisses her temple.

    After a few moments in the embrace, Natasha pulls back slightly and says, “now, why don’t we get all snuggled up and watch a movie?”

    Kate doesn’t need to be told twice and quickly settles herself between Natasha and Wanda, as Natasha picks a movie for them to watch and Wanda wraps them all up in a fluffy blanket.

    Liho must have a sixth sense that some cuddling is occurring as she makes her way over to curl up in Wanda’s lap as Lucky lays underneath all their feet.

    Halfway through the movie, Natasha looks down to see Kate half asleep on her shoulder, so she shifts so that she is laying down and pulls Kate to cuddle up with her. Kate immediately puts her head over Natasha’s heart, lets out a deep sigh, and let’s her eyes slip close, feeling much better than she had when she first woke up.

    Nam prosto nuzhno zaverit' yeye, chto ona v bezopasnosti i lyubima = We just have to reassure her she is safe and loved Thankful for Family Chapter Notes See the end of the chapter for notes The following week, Natasha and Wanda had to frequently remind and reassure Kate that she was only grounded, and that no other punishment was coming. By the end of the week, Kate was more relaxed and her subconscious finally got the fact that she was only grounded.

    That being said, being grounded is boring.

    Kate didn’t want to complain, because this was BY FAR the easiest punishment she has ever had. But by day 5, she had already finished all the books in her room and was two weeks ahead on homework.

    Natasha and Wanda seemed to notice and Natasha took her down to train more often and Wanda let her help make dinner; making the rest of the week past by quicker.

    And before they knew it, it was time for Thanksgiving.

    In years prior, Natasha always spent Thanksgiving with Clint’s family. So, in keeping with tradition, Natasha, Wanda, Kate, Yelena, Lucky, and even Liho, all climbed into the Quinjet early on Thanksgiving morning and headed for the Barton’s Farm.

    Well, not exactly.

    They had to make a stop first.

    Kate was confused when she felt the Quinjet lowering and slowing down to land far sooner than she had anticipated.

    She was just about to ask her parent’s what was wrong when she looks out the window to see two figures standing next to another smaller aircraft.

    As they get closer to the ground, and upon further investigation, Kate realizes that it is none other than Melina and Alexei.

    “Wait,” Kate says, “what is babushka and dedushka doing in the middle of a field?”

    “They ran out of fuel on their way to Barton’s,” Yelena chuckles, “Daddy forgot to fill the tank before they left.”

    “And by the looks of it, Melina seems none too happy about it either,” Wanda adds with a smirk.

    “She is not,” Natasha sighs, rubbing her forehead as she lands the jet, “you should have heard her when she called me this morning.”

    “I didn’t know they were going to Clint’s too,” Kate says with a smile on her face; excited to be able to with most of her family for the holiday.

    “Well, it was going to be a surprise,” Melina says as she walks up the ramp of the jet with a pissed off look on her face, “but someone forgot to refuel the jet before we left.”

    “How many times do I have to say I am sorry, moya lyubov?” Alexei responds pitifully as he walks up behind Melina, carrying their bags.

    Melina rolls her eyes and huffs slightly.

    Kate, on the other hand, quickly jumps out of her seat, runs over to Melina, and wraps her arms around her in a tight hug.

    Melina’s face softens into a warm smile.

    “Hi babushka,” Kate smiles.

    “Hello moya milyy,” Melina sighs lovingly as she returns the hug, her sour mood instantly dissipating.

    Kate leans slowly out of the embrace, “I missed you guys.”

    “We missed you too, molodoy,” Alexei says as he walks over to them.

    Kate smiles and leans over to give him a quick hug.

    “Okay everyone,” Natasha calls back to them, “Mama, Alexei, I know you guys just got on, but we really need to get going if we are gonna make it in time.”

    “Of course, moya bol'shaya devochka,” Melina walks over to give Natasha, Yelena, and Wanda a quick kiss on their temples before sitting in a seat and buckling.

    Once everyone is back in their seats and strapped in, Natasha starts up the Quinjet.

    They land on the farm just as the turkey is being pulled out of the oven. After some quick introductions, the big family sat down to a wonderful feast.

    Much like on Christmas, Kate feels like this is the best Thanksgiving she has had since her dad passed away.

    There is just so much love, joy, and happiness flowing between everyone sitting at the table. Her heart has never felt so full.

    Almost too full.

    After everyone is done eating dinner, Wanda, Melina, Natasha, and Yelena start clearing the dishes while Clint starts to pack the leftovers into containers for everyone. Laura starts to get some of the desserts ready while Alexei falls asleep on the couch and Nate urges his siblings and Kate to go outside and play.

    And after much prodding, and a lot of puppy dog eyes, Nate manages to convince Lila, Kate, and Cooper to go outside to play.

    “Go on ahead, I will catch up with you guys,” Kate says as she sits on the top step of the porch, watching Nate, Lila, and Cooper start to throw around a frisbee.

    She smiles as she thinks about how perfect this day is.

    Perfect.

    Perfect and slightly overwhelming. Scratch that; wicked overwhelming.

    Kate is still not used to family gatherings like this. Her whole family. Together. In one place. And no one is fighting.

    Everything is perfect. And Kate can’t help but hope she won’t do anything to screw this up, or worse, lose this.

    Before she can dwell on her thoughts for too long, she feels someone sit down next to her and bump their shoulder with hers.

    Looking over, she sees Natasha smiling softly down at her.

    “Beautiful day, isn’t it?” Natasha says.

    “Yeah,” Kate replies softly, looking up at the beautiful weather and landscape around them. Natasha continues to look down at her daughter, recognizing the thoughtful look in her eyes, and knows exactly what she is feeling.

    “You know,” Natasha starts softly, “my first few holidays here, I felt the same way. It can be a little overwhelming at times.”

    Kate looks up at Natasha, slightly surprised her mother was able to pinpoint her exact feelings. Natasha is looking back at her with a soft, understanding expression.

    “Especially if you are not used to having holidays like this,” Natasha continues, “it is okay to need a break from time to time.”

    Kate smiles softly before looking down at her hands, “Yeah, it is a little bit overwhelming. Thanksgiving was never like this, for me, in the past,” she pauses looks back up at Natasha. Natasha grabs Kate’s hand and gives it a reassuring squeeze, letting Kate take the time she needs to gather her thoughts before continuing.

    “I guess I am a little scared I am gonna screw everything up or lose this,” Kate smiles sadly as a single tear runs down her face, when were my eyes even watering? , “And I really, really don’t want to lose this.”

    “Oh malyshka,” Natasha says pulling Kate into a tight hug and kissing her temple, “You aren’t going to screw anything up, and I promise to you, you will never lose this.”

    They stay in the embrace for a few moments before Kate slowly pulls away.

    “And I promise these feelings will get better as time goes on,” Natasha continues, wiping away the tear, “but in the meantime, if you ever need a break, just let me know. You don’t need to explain to anyone.”

    Kate nods and smiles softly.

    “Thank you, Mama.”

    “I love you, malyshka.”

    “I love you, too.”

    Just then, Nate runs up and basically tackles Kate in a hug. Natasha chuckles “Hey, Nate-the-great,” Kate laughs.

    “Katie!” Nate says as he pulls back, “Come play frisbee with us! I wanna play teams but we need one more!”

    Kate looks to Natasha, smiles, then pick Nate up and throws him over her shoulder, causing him to squeal and giggle, “Alright then, let’s get a move on then!”

    Natasha shakes her head fondly at the sight.

    A few minutes later, Wanda comes out on the porch and sits next to Natasha in Kate’s previous spot.

    “Everything okay?” she asks Natasha.

    “Yeah,” Natasha smiles softly, putting her head on Wanda’s shoulder, “she was just feeling a bit…”

    “I getcha,” Wanda says, knowing exactly what Natasha meant and exactly how Kate felt.

    They both let out contented sighs and fondly watch the kids have fun in the yard.

    About a half hour or so later, the kids get called in for dessert.

    Everyone reconvenes back at the table and start to load up on multiple different cookies, pies, chocolates, and other sweets.

    Once everyone is done with their dessert, Clint claps his hands, “Alright everyone. It is a Barton family tradition after dessert on Thanksgiving to go around the table and have everyone say at least one thing they are thankful for. I will start. This year, I am thankful for everyone sitting here at this table,” he says with a smile.

    Laura rolls her eyes good naturedly with a small smirk, causing Kate to believe that Clint says this every year.

    “I am thankful for everyone’s health,” Laura says, “and for new members of this family,” she ends smiling at Kate.

    Kate smiles back.

    And thus, everyone goes around the table, citing the things they are thankful for.

    Melina, Alexei, and Yelena mention how they are thankful for reconnecting; with Yelena adding her extreme thankfulness that the Red Room is officially taken down.

    Cooper expresses his thankfulness for his family as well as his friends. Lila says something similar. Nate, on the other hand, says he is thankful for meeting Lucky and Liho, which gives everyone a good laugh.

    Next is Wanda’s turn.

    “Wow, there is just so much I am thankful for this year,” she says smiling at everyone at the table, “Much like Clint, I am thankful for everyone here at this table. But I am especially thankful for my two favorite girls,” she smiles at Natasha and Kate.

    Natasha smiles back, and then its her turn, “I am also thankful for everyone at this table, especially for Wanda; but I think I am thankful, most of all, Kate,” she turns to look at Kate with soft loving gaze, “without you, I wouldn’t have Wanda, or have become a mother. I am so very thankful that you have come into my life, malen’kiy yastreb. I don’t know what I would do without you.”

    Kate can feel tears pooling in her eyes and she blinks quickly to try and get rid of them before they start down her face. She gets up and give Natasha a quick hug before returning to her seat.

    Once she sits back down, she notices everyone’s gaze upon her.

    Oh shit. It’s my turn. How the hell do I even attempt to follow that?

    She is silent for a few moments, collecting her thoughts before she clears her throat.

    “I think, what I am most thankful for this year is that stupid black-market auction,” Kate says, pausing for a moment as everyone looks slightly confused before she continues, “If that auction had never taken place, it would have never been raided by the Tracksuits, I would have never put on the Ronin suit, and I would have never met any of you. That auction ultimately gave me everything. It gave me amazing Aunts and Uncles, and cousins, and friends, and grandparents,” then she looks over at Natasha and Wanda, “And it gave me the parents I have been waiting for. So, yes, this year, I am thankful for that illegal, underground, black-market auction.”

    Natasha reaches out and pulls Kate into a warm hug, kissing her forehead.

    Clint then raises his glass, and everyone follows suit.

    “Happy Thanksgiving everyone!”

    Everyone cheers and clinks their glasses together.

    www.tumblr.com/lucky-bishova-42 See the end of the chapter for more notes On their last day at the farm, after many many MANY attempts, Alexei had finally managed to convince Clint into facing him in a competition. Despite his pleas, however, Clint still refuses to engage in any strength or target competition.

    Instead, Clint offers something that would be more of an even playing field for both of them. A fishing competition.

    So right after lunch, Clint and Alexei head to the dock on the lake, along with Kate and Yelena who decide that they can’t miss what is about to happen. The rest of the family opted to stay at the house; Natasha was watching a movie with Nate and Cooper, Wanda was baking cookies with Lila, and Melina and Laura were relaxing in the kitchen with a cup of tea.

    While Alexei and Clint were prepping and baiting their fishing rods, Kate and Yelena got comfortable sitting at the edge of the dock.

    “So,” Kate speaks up, “what exactly are you guys doing again?”

    “Yeah, like what is the competition? Most fish? Biggest fish? Longest cast?” Yelena asks. Both men just shrug.

    “I dunno,” Clint says, “biggest fish probably.”

    “Da,” Alexei nods, “this makes the most sense.”

    Kate and Yelena chuckle.

    “Are we basing the size off of how it looks or length?” Kate asks, “Or did you bring a scale?” “Probably just looks; I didn’t bring a scale,” Clint says, “I didn’t think that far ahead.” “Of course you didn’t,” Yelena chuckles snarkily. Kate softly elbows her in the side as she tries to contain her own giggles.

    Clint rolls his eyes, and casts his line out into the lake. Alexei following suit. Then they both sit down on the edge of the dock on opposite sides of Yelena and Kate.

    “Huh,” Kate says, “I don’t know why I thought this would be more exciting.”

    The answer is that Alexei has the bright idea to ditch the fishing rods, and just lean over the edge of the dock where they are sitting, to catch the fish barehanded. He rationalizes it by explaining that Clint can use his aiming skills and he can use his brute strength to catch the biggest fish.

    His plan is immediately put into action as Clint agrees. They throw some bait right at the end of the dock, and barely a minute passes before all kinds of fish start to approach.

    “Alright,” Yelena says, “pick out what fish you are both gonna grab, and I will count you down. Ready?”

    Both Clint and Alexei nod.

    “Okay. Three… two… one… GO!”

    Alexei and Clint reach down quickly and try to wrangle the fish. They both manage to get their hands on a fish and lean up to show Kate and Yelena their prizes.

    What they both seem to forget is that the fish continue to wiggle and flop when they are pulled out of the water, maybe even more than when they were in the water.

    …and they are slippery…

    As they are sitting back up, both of the fish that they caught instantaneously slip out of their hands and fly directly into Kate and Yelena.

    “Ahhh! Ew!” Kate shrieks, as a fish lands directly in her lap.

    Unfortunately for Kate, the fish that landed on her lap is slightly larger and is having a harder time escaping back into the lake. Kate, and the fish, continue to thrash about while Kate continues to squeal about the fish in her lap. Clint and Yelena both start to try and help rid her of the scaly menace before Alexei stands up.

    “Don’t worry, molodoy, I will save you!” Alexei says, bending down to assist. He starts to almost push Kate back and forth trying to shake the fish off her lap.

    However, he accidently ends up pushing a little too hard, and both the fish and Kate go flying into the muddy lake.

    That is how Kate ends up soaked, covered in mud, and standing in waist deep water.

    How come every time Dedushka comes up with something fun to do, I have to end up taking a shower.

    Kate sends Alexei a scathing look.

    “I am so, so, so, sorry moya vnuchka,” Alexei apologizes as he reaches down and helps pull Kate out of the water.

    Kate sighs.

    “It is okay, dedushka.”

    Yelena immediately shrugs off her jacket and puts it around Kate’s shoulders then starts to lead her back towards the house.

    As they walk back Clint and Alexei start to clean up their fishing supplies, and Clint starts to chuckle.

    “What?” Alexei asks.

    “Natasha is gonna kill you,” Clint laughs.

    “What happened to you?” Melina asks concernedly when Yelena and Kate walk back into the house. Laura immediately gets up to get Kate a towel.

    “You should ask your husband,” Yelena grunts out.

    Natasha and Wanda, having heard the commotion, come into the kitchen. They both gape at the condition of their daughter.

    “Yeah, I am fine,” Kate replies tiredly, “there was a bit of an accident.”

    Yelena explains sulkily, using her hands for the air quotes.

    “He did WHAT?!?” Natasha exclaims.

    Before anyone could answer, Alexei and Clint walk back into the house.
